Motivation: 现有方法将音乐对齐视为后处理或强制一對一的镜头-音乐映射，忽略了专业剪辑中弹性节奏的特性（快速剪辑对应高能量段落，持续镜头对应低能量小节），无法实现符合音乐动态的弹性多对一对齐。

Method: 提出BEAT框架，包含两个核心组件：1) MuVA，一种紧凑的音乐-视觉对齐编码器，采用Sinkhorn正则化的两阶段学习训练；2) Bar-DP，一种能量自适应动态规划算法，可产生跟随音乐动态的弹性多对一对齐。两者集成到五阶段智能体管道中，用结构化文本信号协调高层创意决策。

Result: 在TrailerArena基准上（包含20+指标、四个维度），BEAT在镜头选择、排序和感知质量方面达到最先进性能，并能端到端生成完整预告片。

Conclusion: 通过弹性对齐机制和五阶段智能体管道的设计，BEAT有效解决了预告片生成中节奏匹配的弹性需求，证明了在跨模态特征基础上协调高层创意决策的可行性和优越性。

Abstract: Automatic movie trailer generation must select shots from a full-length film and synchronize them with background music. Existing methods either relegate music alignment to post-processing or enforce rigid one-to-one shot-music mappings, overlooking that professional editing rhythm is elastic: rapid cuts accompany high-energy passages while sustained shots span quieter bars. We introduce BEAT, a framework that addresses this gap with two core components: MuVA, a compact music-visual alignment encoder trained with Sinkhorn-regularized two-stage learning, and Bar-DP, an energy-adaptive dynamic programming algorithm that produces elastic many-to-one alignments following musical dynamics. These components are integrated into a five-phase agentic pipeline that grounds the core alignment in learned cross-modal features while coordinating higher-level creative decisions through structured text signals. To support comprehensive evaluation, we also introduce TrailerArena, a benchmark with 20+ metrics across four complementary dimensions. On TrailerArena, BEAT achieves state-of-the-art performance across shot selection, ordering, and perceptual quality, while producing fully composed trailers end-to-end.

</details>


### [77] [IPIBench: Evaluating Interactive Proactive Intelligence of MLLMs under Continuous Streams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27074)
*Jinzhao Li,Yinuo Chen,Wenxuan Song,Yijia Lei,Yichi Zhang,Honglei Yan,Panwang Pan,Miao Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出IPIBench基准，用于评估多模态大模型在流式视频场景下的交互式主动智能，并设计IPI-Agent框架来解决现有模型在主动触发和反应-主动协调方面的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准主要研究单轮场景下的反应式或主动式交互，忽略了动态多轮场景中用户可能添加、修改或取消主动请求，同时穿插反应式查询的复杂情况。

Method: 提出了IPIBench基准（覆盖主动监控、主动任务管理和交错反应-主动请求），以及IPI-Agent框架（包含交互控制策略和时间门控机制）。

Result: 评估表明现有MLLM存在主动触发不稳定、反应与主动行为协调弱的问题；IPI-Agent在各项设置中一致提升了现有MLLM的性能。

Conclusion: IPI-Agent作为无需训练的智能体框架，能有效提升MLLM在流式视频场景下的交互式主动智能表现。

Abstract: Recent multimodal large language models (MLLMs) achieve strong performance on reactive question answering, but real-world streaming assistants require proactive reasoning over continuous visual inputs. Existing benchmarks mainly study reactive or proactive interactions in isolated single-turn settings, overlooking dynamic multi-turn scenarios where users may add, modify, or cancel proactive requests alongside interleaved reactive queries. To address this gap, we introduce IPIBench, the first benchmark for evaluating Interactive Proactive Intelligence of MLLMs under streaming video settings. IPIBench covers proactive monitoring, proactive task management, and interleaved reactive-proactive requests. Evaluations on representative MLLMs reveal two major limitations: unstable proactive triggering and weak coordination between reactive and proactive behaviors. We further propose IPI-Agent, a training-free agentic framework with an interaction-control policy and a temporal-gating mechanism for stabilizing proactive triggering and coordinating multi-turn interactions. Experiments show that IPI-Agent consistently improves existing MLLMs across all benchmark settings.

</details>


### [78] [SoftCap: Soft-Budget Control for Diffusion Transformer Acceleration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27075)
*Yuhang Zhang,Junxiang Qiu,Huixia Ben,Zhenhua Tang,Shuo Wang,Yanbin Hao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SoftCap是一种无需额外训练的缓存控制层，通过轨迹漂移观测器评估局部缓存风险，并利用软预算PI控制器动态调整完整步骤触发阈值，在保持相同计算量的前提下，显著提升DiT图像生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Diffusion Transformers（DiTs）迭代去噪过程需要大量计算，现有加速方法依赖固定调度或手动阈值，缺乏自适应机制。

Method: 提出SoftCap控制层，包含轨迹漂移观测器（从轻量级隐藏状态统计量估计局部缓存风险）和软预算PI控制器（根据实际计算量与固定参考配置调整完整步骤触发阈值），实现软上限式预算约束。

Result: 在FLUX.1-dev上，与SpeCa相比，在相近计算量下ImageReward从0.967提升至0.981，LPIPS-Full从0.518降至0.498，且目标扫描诊断显示软上限行为符合预期。

Conclusion: SoftCap通过自适应阈值控制实现了训练免费场景下DiT推理的加速与质量提升，且无需预设固定计算预算。

Abstract: Diffusion Transformers (DiTs) achieve strong visual quality, but their iterative denoising process requires many costly Transformer evaluations. Training-free acceleration methods reduce this cost by caching, forecasting, or verifying intermediate features, yet the runtime decision of when to execute a Full step is often driven by fixed schedules or hand-tuned thresholds. We propose \textbf{SoftCap}, a training-free control layer for cache-based DiT inference. SoftCap couples a Trajectory Drift Observer, which estimates local cache risk from lightweight hidden-state statistics, with a Soft-Budget PI Controller, which adjusts the Full-triggering threshold from realized compute relative to a fixed reference profile. The budget is a soft ceiling: it shapes the threshold but does not require a run to spend a prescribed number of Full evaluations. On FLUX.1-dev, SoftCap improves over SpeCa at a comparable middle-compute operating point, raising ImageReward from 0.967 to 0.981 and reducing LPIPS-Full from 0.518 to 0.498 at nearly identical FLOPs, while target-sweep diagnostics show the intended soft-ceiling behavior as the budget is relaxed.

</details>


### [79] [Semi-Supervised Gaze Estimation via Disentangled Subspace Contrastive Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27080)
*Qida Tan,Hongyu Yang,Wenchao Du*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于解耦子空间对比学习（DSCL）的半监督学习框架，通过Jacobian正则化将特征解耦为俯仰角和偏航角等特定子空间，并利用子空间内的序数排名进行对比学习，在仅用5%-20%标注数据的情况下达到与全监督方法相当的泛化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于外观的视线估计因标注样本有限和数据集多样性不足而泛化能力差，现有弱监督方法虽能生成伪标签但仍有局限性，亟需一种减少人工标注依赖且能增强域泛化的方法。

Method: 1) 采用Jacobian正则化将特征解耦为与视线各分量（俯仰角、偏航角）对应的判别性子空间；2) 在子空间内利用固有的序数排名进行对比学习，从少量标注样本和大量未标注样本中学习鲁棒的视线表征。

Result: 在多个基准上，仅使用20%、10%甚至5%的标注数据，在域内和跨域评估下均达到与全监督方法竞争的性能，且即插即用。

Conclusion: DSCL框架有效利用未标注数据增强了视线估计的域泛化能力，显著降低了人工标注成本。

Abstract: Appearance-based gaze estimation always suffers from poor generalization due to limited annotated samples and insufficient dataset diversity. Leading approaches adopt weakly supervised learning to generate large-scale pseudo-labeled data from unconstrained real-world scenarios, aiming to mitigate the domain shifts. In this work, we devise a simple yet effective semi-supervised learning architecture that leverages unlabeled data to enhance domain generalization, thereby reducing reliance on labor-intensive manual annotations. Our key insight is to impose Jacobian regularization to disentangle feature representations into discriminative subspaces dedicated to specific gaze components, such as pitch and yaw angles. We further exploit the intrinsic ordinal ranking within each subspace for contrastive learning, enabling the model to learn robust gaze representations from a small set of labeled samples and an abundance of unlabeled ones. This ultimately yields our Disentangled Subspace Contrastive Learning (DSCL) framework. Extensive experiments on multiple benchmarks verify that the proposed DSCL is plug-and-play, achieving competitive performance using only 20\%, 10\%, and even 5\% of the annotated data under both in-domain and cross-domain evaluation settings. The public code is available at \href{https://github.com/da60266/DSCL}{https://github.com/da60266/DSCL}.

</details>


### [80] [Pop-Up Distractions Reveal Bag-of-Events Behavior in Video Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27101)
*Oscar Chew,Serhii Honcharenko,Qian-Hui Chen,Patricia Lu,Dishant Zaveri,Khoa D. Doan,Kuan-Hao Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 DistractionBench 来评估视频大语言模型在存在无关片段时能否可靠地将主体与事件关联，发现所有主流模型都存在“事件袋”幻觉问题，即模型将视频视为事件集合而非时序序列，导致错误地将广告中的动作归属于主视频主体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频大语言模型虽能理解视频内容，但尚不清楚它们是否真正具备将主体与事件跨时间可靠关联的能力，尤其是在存在无关干扰片段时。

Method: 构建 DistractionBench 基准，通过在长视频中插入短广告等无关片段进行受控干扰实验，量化模型将不同片段实体错误关联的幻觉程度，并定义“事件袋”行为指标。

Result: 评估 11 种主流 VideoLLM，所有模型均表现出显著的事件袋行为，即无法区分不同视频片段中的主体-事件关系，频繁将广告中的动作错误归因于主视频主体。

Conclusion: 当前 VideoLLM 缺乏可靠的时间接地机制，需要开发具有更强主体-事件时序关联能力的模型。

Abstract: A key capability for video understanding is reliably linking subjects to events across time, yet whether Video Large Language Models (VideoLLMs) actually achieve this remains unclear. In this work, we introduce DistractionBench to evaluate whether VideoLLMs can robustly link subjects and events in the presence of unrelated video segments. Through controlled interventions, such as inserting short advertisement clips into longer videos, we show that VideoLLMs frequently hallucinate interactions between entities from different segments, incorrectly attributing actions from injected advertisements to subjects in the main video. We characterize this systematic hallucination as bag-of-events (BoE) behavior, where models process videos as collections of events rather than temporally structured sequences. Evaluating 11 popular VideoLLMs, we find that all models exhibit substantial BoE behavior. Our findings suggest that VideoLLMs lack reliable mechanisms for temporal grounding and motivate the development of models with more robust subject-event association.

</details>


### [81] [COVD: Continual Open-Vocabulary Object Detection with Novel Concept Injection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27116)
*Yupeng Zhang,Ruize Han,Yuzhong Feng,Zixin Ren,Yuntong Tian,Liang Wan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新任务——持续开放词汇目标检测（COVD），用于模型在不完全重训练的情况下持续学习新概念，并设计了NoIn-Det框架，通过冻结视觉编码器、仅更新少量文本分支参数实现高效的概念注入，在Novel-114基准上表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有OVD模型无法应对不断演变的新概念，而重复全量重训练成本过高；同时发现视觉编码器已能感知新概念，瓶颈在于视觉与文本之间的语义对齐不稳定。

Method: 提出NoIn-Det框架：冻结视觉编码器，保留常见概念和已注入概念的文本表示空间，仅通过更新少量文本分支参数来注入新概念，无需额外参数。

Result: 在Novel-114基准上，NoIn-Det有效学习新概念、保留旧知识，且性能持续优于现有VLM持续学习方法，不引入额外参数。

Conclusion: NoIn-Det实现了高效、参数经济的持续开放词汇目标检测，验证了仅更新文本分支即可实现稳定语义对齐的有效性。

Abstract: Open-vocabulary object detection (OVD) has made significant progress, enabling detectors to generalize from seen to unseen categories. However, real-world category spaces continually evolve, and existing OVD models still struggle with newly emerging concepts, while repeated full retraining is prohibitively expensive. To this end, we introduce a new task setting, termed Continual OVD with Novel Concept Injection (COVD), where models sequentially learn incoming novel concept groups while preserving prior concepts and original open-vocabulary knowledge, along with a new benchmark, Novel-114. Our key observation is that pretrained visual encoders often already perceive and represent many novel concepts, and the main bottleneck lies in the lack of stable semantic alignment between visual representations and textual concepts. Based on this, we propose NoIn-Det, an efficient continual injection framework without additional parameters. NoIn-Det freezes the visual encoder, preserves the text representation space using only texts of common concepts and previously injected concepts, and injects novel concepts by updating only a small subset of text-branch parameters beneficial to novel concept learning. Extensive experiments show that NoIn-Det effectively learns novel concepts, preserves old knowledge, and consistently outperforms existing continual learning methods for VLMs without introducing additional parameters.Novel-114 and the code will be released.

</details>


### [82] [Image Thresholding: Understanding Bias of Evaluation Metrics towards Specific Evaluation Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27132)
*Eslam Hegazy,Mohamed Gabr*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究揭示多级图像阈值分割中存在评估偏差：Otsu准则与SSIM/PSNR指标高度相关，而Kapur熵相关性较弱，导致评估框架对Otsu存在天然偏向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上使用SSIM和PSNR评价阈值分割质量时，假设这些指标能无偏地反映分割质量，但该假设从未被验证。本研究旨在检验阈值目标函数与质量指标之间的相关性，揭示潜在的评价偏差。

Method: 在BSDS500数据集上，遍历所有可能的阈值组合，计算Otsu类间方差和Kapur熵与SSIM、PSNR之间的相关性，进行系统性对比分析。

Result: Otsu准则与SSIM和PSNR均呈现高度一致的相关性；Kapur熵的相关性较弱且波动较大。Otsu在所有图像上与PSNR的相关性均优于Kapur，在91%以上的图像上与SSIM的相关性也更优。

Conclusion: 存在固有的指标-目标函数偏差，当前评估框架对Otsu有偏向性。需要建立更中立的评估框架，并将分析扩展到更多阈值准则和应用领域。

Abstract: Multilevel image thresholding is widely used for segmentation in applications ranging from medical imaging to remote sensing. Classical objective functions, such as Otsu's between-class variance and Kapur's entropy, are often optimized using metaheuristic algorithms, with performance evaluated via metrics like Structural Similarity Index (SSIM) and Peak Signal-to-Noise Ratio (PSNR). These evaluations implicitly assume that SSIM and PSNR provide unbiased measures of segmentation quality. In this study, we examine this assumption by analyzing the correlation between thresholding objective functions and quality metrics across all possible thresholds for images in the BSDS500 dataset. Results show that Otsu's criterion consistently exhibits high correlation with both SSIM and PSNR, while Kapur's entropy demonstrates weaker and more variable correlation. Otsu outperforms Kapur in correlation with PSNR for all images and with SSIM for over 91%. Our findings reveal an inherent metric-objective-function bias. This work highlights the need for more neutral evaluation frameworks and motivates extending the analysis to additional thresholding criteria and domains. Source code of this paper can be found at https://w3id.org/met-dp/icpr26-95

</details>


### [83] [Leveraging Visual Signals for Robust Token-Level Uncertainty in Vision-Language Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27136)
*Joseph Hoche,David Brellmann,Gianni Franchi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出VIG-TUQ，一种通过视觉置信度加权的无训练不确定性量化框架，显著提升了多模态大模型的不确定性估计性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LVLM不确定性量化方法多从LLM领域移植，仅关注语言模态，忽视了视觉信息对不确定性的贡献。

Method: 分析视觉特征整合后的隐藏表示，发现高置信度预测更依赖视觉内容；据此设计VIG-TUQ，将词级语言不确定性乘以视觉置信度分数进行加权。

Result: 在多种数据集和LVLM架构（早期融合、晚期融合、原生融合）上，VIG-TUQ通常优于现有词级不确定性方法。

Conclusion: 视觉信息对LVLM不确定性估计有重要影响，显式融入视觉依据可有效提升UQ性能。

Abstract: Uncertainty quantification (UQ) remains a critical challenge in Large Vision Language Models (LVLMs) for reliable predictions and real-world deployment. However, most existing methods are adapted from the LLM literature and primarily focus on the language modality, leaving the contribution of visual information to LVLM uncertainty largely underexplored. In this paper, we investigate how LVLMs process visual information and whether this process can be used to improve uncertainty estimation. By analyzing hidden representations after the integration of visual features during the generation process, we observe that high-confidence predictions rely more heavily on visual content than uncertain ones. Building on this insight, we propose Visual-Grounded Token UQ (VIG-TUQ), a training-free framework that explicitly incorporates visual grounding into uncertainty estimation by weighting token-level language uncertainty with visual grounding scores. We evaluate VIG-TUQ on multiple datasets and across diverse LVLM architectures, including early-fusion, late-fusion, and native-fusion models. Results indicate that our method often improves upon existing token-level uncertainty approaches. Code and data will be made available upon acceptance.

</details>


### [84] [Is an Image Also Worth 16x16=256 Superpixels? A Framework for Attentional Image Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27144)
*Pedro Henrique da Costa Avelar,Anderson R. Tavares,Luís C. Lamb*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Superpixel Transformers (SPT)，统一了超像素图像分类与Vision Transformers，通过多维正余弦位置编码和增强补丁数据结构，在多个数据集上优于超像素GNN方法且与ViT性能相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GNN、超像素和Transformer三者之间缺乏有效的协同连接，且现有超像素GNN方法（如SICGAT）存在像素聚合过程中的信息丢失问题。

Method: 提出SPT框架，支持任意超像素切分策略、连接图和位置编码；引入多维正余弦位置编码和包含超像素形状与颜色信息的增强补丁数据结构。

Result: 在CIFAR10、FashionMNIST和Imagenette数据集上，SPT优于此前超像素GNN方法，且与ViT性能保持竞争力。

Conclusion: SPT弥合了超像素方法与Transformer模型之间的鸿沟，为混合注意力框架和跨域泛化开辟了新路径。

Abstract: Superpixel-based image classification has traditionally leveraged graph neural networks (GNNs) for processing irregular image representations. Recent advances in computer vision, driven by Vision Transformers (ViTs), have introduced new paradigms in self-attentional models, surpassing convolutional neural networks (CNNs) in various tasks. However, a synergistic connection between GNNs, superpixels, and transformers remains unexplored. In this work, we propose Superpixel Transformers (SPT), a novel framework that unifies superpixel-based image classification and ViTs. SPT generalizes the Superpixel Image Classification with Graph Attention Networks (SICGAT) model and ViT to support arbitrary superpixel-based chunking strategies, connectivity graphs, and positional encodings. We introduce refinements including a multidimensional sine-cosine positional encoding and an enriched patch data structure that fully incorporates superpixel shape and color information. By testing SPT across datasets such as CIFAR10, FashionMNIST, and Imagenette, with various superpixel generation and graph connectivity strategies, we demonstrate that SPT achieves superior performance compared to previous superpixel-based GNN methods and remains competitive with ViTs. Notably, our approach addresses the limitations of SICGAT, such as information loss during pixel aggregation, and shows how constrained graph connectivity can enhance ViT performance. SPT bridges the gap between superpixel-based and transformer models, opening avenues for cross-domain generalization and future innovations in hybrid attentional frameworks, and showing that an image can also be worth $16\times16$ superpixels.

</details>


### [85] [Chaos-SSL: An Attention-Based Self-Supervised Learning Framework with Chaotic Transformation for Medical Image Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27146)
*Joao Batista Florindo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Chaos-SSL框架，使用一维混沌映射（Logistic、Tent、Sine）作为自监督学习的非线性数据增强策略，并通过注意力融合机制将混沌预训练特征与ImageNet预训练特征结合，在皮肤病和视网膜病变分类任务上取得了SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准自监督学习仅依赖简单的几何和颜色增强，难以捕获医学图像中细粒度、复杂的纹理细节，导致对微小病变的分类效果不佳。

Method: 两阶段框架：第一阶段利用一维混沌映射（Logistic、Tent、Sine）作为复杂非线性增强，生成更难、语义更丰富的视图进行对比学习；第二阶段通过注意力融合模型，动态结合Chaos-SSL的专用特征与ImageNet预训练的通用特征。

Result: 使用Tent映射预训练30轮+注意力融合，在ISIC 2018上准确率0.9261，在APTOS 2019上准确率0.8726，显著优于现有SSL方法。

Conclusion: 混沌映射作为一种复杂非线性增强策略，能有效提升自监督学习在医学图像细粒度分类中的表现，注意力融合进一步提升了特征表示的泛化能力。

Abstract: Self-Supervised Learning (SSL) has emerged as a powerful paradigm to mitigate the reliance on large, annotated datasets, a common bottleneck in medical image analysis. However, standard SSL methods, which rely on simple geometric and color augmentations, may fail to capture the fine-grained, complex textural details necessary for classifying subtle pathologies. This paper introduces Chaos-SSL, a novel two-stage framework for medical image classification. In the first stage, we propose a new self-supervised pre-training strategy that leverages 1D chaotic maps (Logistic, Tent, and Sine) as a complex, non-linear augmentation for contrastive learning. We hypothesize that these chaotic transformations create ``harder'' and more semantically-rich views, forcing a network to learn robust representations of fine-grained medical textures. In the second stage, we introduce an attention-based fusion model that dynamically combines the specialized features from our Chaos-SSL model with the general-purpose features of a larger, ImageNet-pre-trained model. We validate our method on two public datasets: ISIC 2018 (skin lesions) and APTOS 2019 (diabetic retinopathy). Our results demonstrate that the Chaos-SSL model pre-trained with a Tent map for 30 epochs, followed by attention fusion, achieves performance fully competitive with the state-of-the-art, yielding an accuracy of 0.9261 on ISIC 2018 and 0.8726 on APTOS 2019. This significantly outperforms existing SSL methods, including several recent approaches.

</details>


### [86] [Touch-R1: Reinforcing Touch Reasoning in MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27154)
*Yingxin Lai,Yafei Zhou,Fucai Zhu,Siyu Zhu,Weihao Yuan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TouchReason-1M大规模触觉数据集和TouchReason-Bench评估框架，并基于此训练了Touch-R1触觉推理多模态大模型，通过触觉增强的GRPO训练目标，在触觉推理和视觉-触觉冲突解决任务上显著超越现有方法，展现出类似R1的物理推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于规则的强化学习已推动多模态模型在视觉推理上的发展，但触觉推理领域尚未被充分探索。已有触觉-语言模型多依赖监督或对比学习目标，无法将预测基于物理证据或纠正误导性的视觉先验。触觉推理面临两个特定模态的挑战：物理属性的序数特性（如硬度、粗糙度）以及光学触觉传感器的跨传感器分布偏移。

Method: 提出Touch-R1模型，基于Qwen2.5-VL-7B，采用触觉增强的GRPO（Tactile-grounded GRPO）训练目标。该目标结合了四个奖励成分：序数感知准确性、跨传感器物理一致性、结构化格式控制，以及输入侧触觉建模范式。其中触觉使用奖励在真实触觉输入比反事实控制（移除、打乱或噪声遮蔽触觉流）更优时才赋予奖励。

Result: 在TouchReason-Bench上，Touch-R1-7B平均性能比Octopi-13B高出18.4%，比GPT-4o高出24.7%。其结构化推理轨迹展现出探测、比较和修正等涌现行为，证明了R1风格推理可以有效地建立在物理接触基础之上。

Conclusion: 触觉推理可以通过规则强化学习有效实现，Touch-R1展示了物理接触可以像视觉推理一样支持结构化、可解释的推理过程，为多模态触觉推理开辟了新方向。

Abstract: While rule-based reinforcement learning has recently catalyzed explicit reasoning in multimodal models, tactile reasoning remains largely underexplored. Existing tactile-language models primarily rely on supervised or contrastive objectives, which limits their capacity to ground predictions in physical evidence or rectify misleading visual priors. Tactile reasoning introduces two modality-specific challenges: the ordinal nature of physical attributes (e.g., hardness, roughness) and the cross-sensor distribution shifts inherent in optical tactile hardware. In this work, we introduce TouchReason-1M, a large-scale multimodal dataset comprising over 1M synchronized tactile pairs across four distinct sensors, and TouchReason-Bench, a rigorous framework for evaluating tactile perception and visual-tactile conflict resolution. Building upon these, we propose Touch-R1, a tactile reasoning MLLM based on Qwen2.5-VL-7B. Touch-R1 is trained via a tactile-grounded GRPO objective that combines ordinal-aware accuracy, cross-sensor physical consistency, structured-format control, and an input-side tactile grounding objective. Specifically, the tactile-use reward assigns credit only when authentic tactile inputs yield superior correctness relative to counterfactual controls where the tactile stream is removed, shuffled, or noise-masked. On TouchReason-Bench, Touch-R1-7B outperforms Octopi-13B by 18.4\% and GPT-4o by 24.7\% on average. Its structured reasoning traces reveal emergent behaviors of probing, comparison, and revision, demonstrating that R1-style reasoning can be effectively grounded in physical contact.

</details>


### [87] [Semantic Robustness Probing via Inpainting: An Interactive Tool for Safety-Critical Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27155)
*Nico Steckhan,Krutarth Prajapati,Weija Shao,Silvia Vock*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SemProbe是一个用于评估目标检测器语义鲁棒性的工具，通过扩散模型进行受控图像修复，支持批量处理、可配置生成参数和自动模型推理，生成可追溯的鲁棒性证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全关键领域的目标检测器需要超越像素级扰动的语义上有意义的测试方法，现有方法缺乏对语义鲁棒性的系统评估工具。

Method: 用户上传部署图像，手动或自动创建掩码，选择操作设计域因素或自定义提示，通过扩散模型进行受控图像修复。系统支持批量作业、并行种子/工作流变体和可配置生成参数，自动运行模型推理并显示带注释的前后对比与性能差异。

Result: 所有探针作为结构化工件记录，提供可追溯的鲁棒性证据，并在手部检测场景（维度锯）上进行了演示，针对保险导向测试标准中的因素。

Conclusion: SemProbe能够与安全评估工作流对齐，生成可追溯的语义鲁棒性证据，为安全关键领域的目标检测器测试提供实用工具。

Abstract: Testing object detectors in safety-critical domains requires semantically meaningful probes beyond pixel-level corruptions. We present SemProbe, a tool for semantic robustness probing: users upload deployment images, create masks manually or automatically, select operational design domain-derived factors (or custom prompts), and run diffusion-based controlled inpainting. The system supports batch jobs, parallel seed/workflow variations, and configurable generation parameters. After each output, model inference runs automatically and displays annotated before/after comparisons with performance deltas. All probes are logged as structured artifacts, enabling traceable robustness evidence aligned with safety evaluation workflows. We demonstrate \textsc{SemProbe} on hand detection for dimension saws, targeting factors from insurance-oriented test criteria.

</details>


### [88] [Generative Animations: A Multi-Model Pipeline for Prompt-Driven Motion Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27203)
*Mannat Khurana,Sanyam Jain,Rishav Agarwal*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Generative Animations系统，通过LLM语义解析+SAM视觉定位，将自然语言提示自动转化为生产级动画路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手动创建自定义动画路径繁琐低效，需要设计师手动选择预设、绘制贝塞尔点和配置时序属性。

Method: 链式调用大语言模型进行语义解析，结合Segment Anything Model进行视觉定位，自动生成尊重场景几何、处理深度遮挡和3D透视变换的运动路径。

Result: 在三个用例中验证：轮廓跟随轨迹、带z-order感知的轨道动画、变换对象上的透视对齐运动。

Conclusion: 该系统能将自然语言直接转化为生产级动画，大幅降低动画创作门槛。

Abstract: Animation elevates digital documents into immersive experiences, yet creating custom motion paths remains cumbersome, requiring designers to manually select presets, plot Bézier points, and configure timing properties. We introduce Generative Animations, a system that transforms natural language prompts into production-ready animations. By chaining Large Language Models (LLMs) for semantic parsing with the Segment Anything Model (SAM) for visual grounding, our pipeline automatically generates motion paths that respect scene geometry, handle depth-based occlusions, and honor 3D perspective transforms. We demonstrate the system through three use cases: contour-following trajectories, orbital animations with z-order awareness, and perspective-aligned motion on transformed objects.

</details>


### [89] [MRT: Masked Region Transformer for Layered Image Generation and Editing at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27235)
*Zhicong Tang,Zhao Zhang,Jingye Chen,Mohan Zhou,Yifan Pu,Yuchi Liu,Yalong Bai,Ethan Smith,Yuhui Yuan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MRT是一个200亿参数的掩码区域扩散模型，用于多层透明图像生成与编辑，在三个互补任务上显著超越现有方法（包括商用系统），并实现10-100倍更快的推理速度和50-90%的GPU内存节省。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多层图像生成与编辑是类似自然语言中单词级编辑的基础能力，但目前在大规模场景下仍未被充分探索，现有方法难以实现灵活的图层级重用、编辑和组合。

Method: 1) 将文本到图层、图像到图层、图层到图层三个任务统一到共享的掩码区域扩散框架中，通过选择性令牌掩码实现灵活生成与编辑；2) 引入溢出感知画布层，处理边界不一致并支持半透明背景合成，实现超出可见画布边界的完整可编辑图层；3) 应用扩散蒸馏实现8步实时多层生成。

Result: 在三个任务上均大幅超越先前最优方法（包括多个商用系统）；在图像到图层任务上显著优于Qwen-Image-Layered模型；推理速度提升10-100倍，激活GPU内存消耗减少50-90%。

Conclusion: MRT为多层透明图像生成建立了新的基准，证明大规模掩码区域扩散模型在图层级视觉内容生成与编辑上的有效性。

Abstract: Layered image generation and editing is a fundamental capability that enables layer-wise reuse, editing, and composition of generated visual content, analogous to word-level editing in natural language. Despite its importance, this remains an underexplored area at scale. To address this gap, we present MRT, a 20B-parameter masked region diffusion model tailored for multi-layer transparent image generation and editing, trained on over 10M multilingual design samples spanning diverse aspect ratios and textual prompts. To fully leverage this scale, we make two key technical contributions. First, we unify three complementary tasks including text-to-layers, image-to-layers, and layers-to-layers within a shared masked region diffusion framework, where selective token masking enables flexible layer-wise generation and editing. Second, to enable overflow layer generation, we introduce an overflow-aware canvas layer that handles boundary inconsistencies and supports semi-transparent background synthesis, enabling complete editable layers extending beyond visible canvas boundaries. Additionally, we apply diffusion distillation to achieve 8-step, real-time multi-layer generation with minimal quality degradation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our framework substantially outperforms prior state-of-the-art approaches, including various commercial systems, across all three tasks, establishing a new benchmark for multi-layer transparent image generation. Notably, our model significantly outperforms the concurrent Qwen-Image-Layered model in image-to-layers quality according to user-study results, while achieving 10-100\times faster inference and reducing activation GPU memory consumption by 50-90\% during image-to-layer inference.

</details>


### [90] [Can Retrieval Heads See Images? Multimodal Retrieval Heads in Long-Context Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27243)
*Aaron Branson Cigres Li,Zhaowei Wang,Yu Zhao,Yiming Du,Haobo Li,Xiyu Ren,Ginny Wong,Simon See,Lishu Luo,Haodong Duan,Pasquale Minervini,Yangqiu Song*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种多模态检索头检测方法，发现大型视觉语言模型中仅4.4-10.2%的注意力头贡献了50%的检索分数，这些头对多文档检索和视觉问答至关重要，并可直接用于提升文档检索性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究在大语言模型中用检索头研究长上下文行为，但其基于文本复制的标准不适用于图像证据。因此需要一种能处理文本和视觉证据的多模态检索头检测方法。

Method: 提出多模态检索头检测方法，通过从问题token到文本或视觉证据的注意力分数进行评分；识别出稀疏的关键注意力头，并分析其跨模态共享性和模态内动态性。

Result: 仅4.4-10.2%的注意力头贡献50%检索分数；屏蔽top-5%检索头导致MMLongBench-Doc从48.2%降至5.7%，SlideVQA从71.2%降至8.9%；Qwen3-VL-8B在MMDocIR上Recall@1提升7.7/7.4（页面检索）和6.3/6.8（布局检索）。

Conclusion: 多模态检索头具有稀疏性、内在性和因果重要性，且无需额外训练即可直接用于视觉丰富文档的排序检索。

Abstract: Large vision-language models increasingly rely on long-context modeling to reason over documents, hour-level videos, and long-horizon agent trajectories, requiring them to locate relevant evidence across interleaved text and images. Prior work has studied this behavior using retrieval heads in large language models, but its copy-based criterion does not directly apply when evidence appears in images. We introduce a multimodal retrieval head detection method that scores attention from question tokens to textual or visual evidence. With this method, we show that multimodal retrieval heads are sparse, intrinsic, and causally important: only 4.4-10.2% of attention heads account for 50% of the positive retrieval-score mass, and masking the top-5% selected heads drops MMLongBench-Doc from 48.2% to 5.7% and SlideVQA from 71.2% to 8.9%, while random-head masking is far less damaging. Further analysis shows that these heads are partly shared across modalities yet remain dynamic within each modality, with image retrieval heads changing more than text retrieval heads as context length and haystack modality change. Without further training, we find that these heads can also be used directly to rank visually rich documents: on MMDocIR, Qwen3-VL-8B selected-head scoring improves Recall@1 by 7.7/7.4 macro/micro points for page retrieval and 6.3/6.8 points for layout retrieval over the strongest reported baseline.

</details>


### [91] [A Dynamic Programming Framework for Discovering Count and Values of Multilevel Image Thresholding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27287)
*Eslam Hegazy,Mohamed Gabr*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于动态规划和改进最小错误阈值准则的多级图像阈值分割方法（MET-DP），能自动确定最优阈值数量，在处理大量阈值时比传统方法更快，但生成的图像在SSIM和PSNR指标上略低于需手动输入阈值数量的传统方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大多数阈值分割方法需要用户手动输入阈值数量，而能自动确定合适阈值数量的方法更具优势，因此本文旨在开发一种自动确定阈值数量的高效阈值分割方法。

Method: 基于动态规划算法，并修改了最小错误阈值（MET）准则，通过经验统计研究分析方法的优越性，并在自然图像、卫星图像和医学图像等综合数据集上与多种先进方法进行对比。

Result: MET-DP方法在高阈值数量情况下比传统动态规划阈值方法耗时更少；能自动检测出大多数测试图像的合适阈值数量；但传统需要输入阈值数量的方法生成的图像在SSIM和PSNR指标上优于MET-DP。

Conclusion: MET-DP是一种能自动确定阈值数量且计算效率高的图像阈值分割方法，适用于多种类型图像，但在图像质量指标上仍有提升空间。

Abstract: Multilevel Image thresholding is an important preprocessing algorithm in computer vision applications nowadays. Since most common thresholding methods take the desired count of thresholds as input by the user, thresholding methods that automatically determines a suitable count of thresholds from the input image itself are advantageous. In this article, a novel thresholding method based on a dynamic programming algorithm and a modification of Minimum Error Thresholding (MET) criterion is thoroughly presented. An empirical statistical study is performed to pinpoint why this proposed method is superior. Moreover, an extended comparison between this proposed method and other state-of-the-art methods is performed on a comprehensive set of natural, satellite and medical test images. The numerical results show that the proposed MET-DP method takes much less time than traditional dynamic programming thresholding methods when the number of thresholds is high. The proposed method can detect a suitable count of thresholds for most of tested images of different types. However, traditional methods that take the count of thresholds as input produce thresholded images of higher structural similarity index measure (SSIM) and peak signal-to-noise ratio (PSNR) values than MET-DP. Source code can be found on https://w3id.org/met-dp/article1-code

</details>


### [92] [Gemini Embedding 2: A Native Multimodal Embedding Model from Gemini](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27295)
*Madhuri Shanbhogue,Zhe Li,Shanfeng Zhang,Gustavo Hernández Ábrego,Shih-Cheng Huang,Aashi Jain,Daniel Salz,Sonam Goenka,Chaitra Hegde,Ji Ma,Feiyang Chen,Jiaxing Wu,Tanmaya Dabral,Babak Samari,Kevin Poulet,Daniel Cer,Kaifeng Chen,Paul Suganathan,Hui Hui,Jovan Andonov,Philippe Schlattner,Jay Han,Iftekhar Naim,Wing Lowe,Vladimir Pchelin,Albert Yang,Yi-Ting Chen,Zhongli Ding,Grace Zhang,Georg Heigold,Yichang Chen,Antoine Reveillon,Brendan Mccloskey,Wenlei Zhou,Dahun Kim,Rui Meng,Emma Wang,Jack Zheng,Halley Fede,Zhen Yang,Keegan Mosley,Brian Potetz,Sahil Dua,Henrique Schechter Vera,Shen Gao,Hesen Zhang,Andreas Hess,Hengxuan Ying,Alberto Montes,Karan Gill,Min Choi,Sebastian Russo,Anja Hauth,Jinhyuk Lee,Michael Boratko,Megan Barnes,Vikram Rao,Claudiu Musat,Cyril Allauzen,Ehsan Variani,Shankar Kumar,Tom Bagby,Junyi Jiao,Yang Gu,Tengxin Li,Ayush Agrawal,Roberto Santana,Dev Nath,Stephen Karukas,Shuoxuan Han,Lucia Loher,Alice Twu,Nidhi Vyas,Siddharth Bhai,Frank Palma Gomez,Wangyuan Zhang,Chaoren Liu,Jizheng Yang,Steve Qiu,Shijie Zhang,Sujay Kulkarni,Sascha Rothe,Sean Nakamoto,Raphael Hoffmann,Zach Gleicher,Yunhsuan Sung,Qin Yin,Tom Duerig,Mojtaba Seyedhosseini*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Gemini Embedding 2 是一个原生多模态嵌入模型，能将视频、音频、图像和文本统一嵌入到同一表示空间，在多模态检索任务上达到SOTA水平，且零样本能力强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的嵌入模型通常只处理单一模态（如文本或图像），缺乏统一处理视频、音频、图像和文本等多模态信息的能力，难以满足实际应用中跨模态检索和多模态融合的需求。

Method: 基于Gemini的多模态能力，采用多任务多阶段训练的大规模对比学习框架，将视频、音频、图像和文本嵌入到统一的表示空间中，支持任意组合的交错输入。

Result: 在MSCOCO上R@1达62.9，Vatex上NDCG@10达68.8，MTEB多语言达69.9，MTEB代码达84.0，超越多个专用模型。

Conclusion: Gemini Embedding 2 是一个统一的多模态嵌入模型，在多种检索任务上超越专用模型，零样本泛化能力强，适用于RAG、推荐和搜索等下游场景。

Abstract: We introduce Gemini Embedding 2, a native multimodal embedding model that allows embedding video, audio, image, and text modalities in a unified representation space. We leverage the multimodal capabilities of Gemini to produce embeddings for arbitrary combinations of interleaved inputs across all these modalities that generalize well across a wide variety of tasks. Applying large-scale contrastive learning in a multi-task multi-stage training setup, we achieve state-of-the-art performance on key embedding benchmarks including unimodal, cross-modal, and multimodal retrieval spanning a diverse set of tasks. We show that our embedding model demonstrates strong performance (with a score of 62.9 R@1 on MSCOCO, 68.8 NDCG@10 on Vatex, 69.9 on MTEB multilingual and 84.0 on MTEB Code) across a variety of tasks surpassing the performance of specialized models. These unified capabilities make Gemini Embedding 2 a promising candidate for downstream use cases such as RAG, recommendation and search. Furthermore, its robust zero-shot performance across distinct fields - from astronomy and bioscience to fine arts and the culinary arts - establishes it as a highly reliable, out-of-the-box representation even for specialized domains.

</details>


### [93] [How and What to Imagine? Visual Thinking in Unified Multimodal Models for Cross-View Spatial Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27310)
*Qian Yang,Ankur Sikarwar,Huy Le,Le Zhang,Zhuan Shi,Perouz Taslakian,Aishwarya Agrawal*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出View Dropout (VDrop)训练策略，结合全景视觉思维，让统一多模态模型在跨视角空间推理中真正利用中间思考图像，在五个真实世界跨域基准上取得最佳泛化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉-语言模型在跨视角空间推理中存在缺陷，往往依赖语言推理而忽略中间思考图像中的细粒度几何信息，需要让视觉思维真正发挥作用。

Method: 提出View Dropout (VDrop)训练干预，在输入视图中隐藏部分区域不让答案看到，迫使模型依赖思考图像进行回答；并对比三种视觉思维变体（俯视图、全景图、点匹配图），在合成场景上训练并在五个真实世界基准上评估。

Result: 全景视觉思维搭配VDrop是唯一兼具可学习性和信息性的配置，在五个真实世界跨域基准上取得最佳泛化性能。

Conclusion: 有效的视觉思维需要平衡可学习性与信息性，全景图是最优的中间表征形式，VDrop能有效促使模型利用视觉思维而非仅依赖语言推理。

Abstract: Cross-view spatial reasoning remains a weak spot for vision-language models (VLMs): they often reason in language and lose the fine-grained geometry needed for the task. Thinking with images aims to address this by generating an intermediate thinking image, but recent work shows that models often ignore the visual evidence in these traces. We therefore ask how to make visual thinking matter, and what kind of visual thinking works best. We study these questions in unified multimodal models (UMMs), which natively support interleaved image-text generation. For the first question, we propose View Dropout (VDrop), a training-time intervention that hides parts of one input view from the answer span while keeping them visible to the thinking-image tokens. This encourages the model to use the thinking image when answering, instead of relying only on the input views. Once the thinking image is used for answer prediction, we study which type of visual thinking is most effective. We frame this as a learnability-informativeness tradeoff and compare three thinking-image variants: top-down, panoramic, and point-matching renderings. Trained on synthetic scenes and evaluated on five real-world out-of-domain benchmarks, panoramic visual thinking with VDrop is the only configuration that is both informative and learnable, and it achieves the best out-of-domain generalization.

</details>


### [94] [Q-GeoMem: Question-Guided Geometric Memory for Video Spatial Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27318)
*Xianqiang Gao,Qizhi Chen,Delin Qu,Haoming Song,Zhigang Wang,Bin Zhao,Dong Wang,Xuelong Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种问题引导的几何记忆框架（Ours），用于视频空间推理，通过注入相机条件的几何信息并维护细粒度上下文库和语义-几何证据库两种互补记忆，结合Q-Former的问题相关性与新颖性评分机制，在VSI-Bench和VSTI-Bench上取得了最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频空间语言模型虽然提升了几何感知和长程上下文建模能力，但往往将记忆视为通用时间缓存，会引入冗余或无关的几何信息，削弱长程推理能力。

Method: 1) 将相机条件几何注入视觉token；2) 维护两种互补记忆：细粒度上下文库（保留近期密集特征和相机状态）和语义-几何证据库（保留紧凑的长程证据）；3) 通过Q-Former计算候选帧的问题相关性与新颖性乘积作为评分，并采用容量替换规则保持记忆紧凑；4) 推理时先读取两个记忆，再自适应融合当前帧表示。

Result: 在VSI-Bench和VSTI-Bench上，该方法在评估的空间推理模型中达到了最先进性能，消融实验验证了所提证据评分机制的有效性。

Conclusion: 问题引导的几何记忆框架能有效提升视频空间推理能力，证明了选择性记忆机制在长程几何推理中的重要性。

Abstract: Video spatial reasoning requires accumulating viewpoint-dependent evidence over time while retaining information useful to the question being asked. Existing spatial video-language models improve geometric perception and long-range context modeling, but often treat memory as a generic temporal cache, which can introduce redundant or irrelevant geometry and weaken long-horizon reasoning. We propose \textbf{\ours}, a question-guided geometric memory framework for video spatial reasoning. \ours injects camera-conditioned geometry into visual tokens and maintains two complementary memories: a Fine-Grained Context Bank for recent dense features and camera states, and a Semantic-Geometric Evidence Bank for compact long-range evidence. Each candidate frame is scored by the product of Q-Former-based question relevance and novelty with respect to the retained bank; this score is stored and reused during reading, while a capacity-based replacement rule keeps the bank compact. During reasoning, both memories are read before update and adaptively fused with the current frame representation. Experiments on VSI-Bench and VSTI-Bench show that \ours achieves state-of-the-art performance among evaluated spatial reasoning models, validating the effectiveness of question-guided geometric memory. Ablations further verify the contribution of the proposed evidence scoring mechanism.

</details>


### [95] [PARE: Pruning and Adaptive Routing for Efficient Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27336)
*Yutong Wang,Yunke Wang,Tianfan Xue,Yu Qiao,Yaohui Wang,Xinyuan Chen,Chang Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PARE通过结构感知剪枝和输入自适应路由，在视频DiTs中同时压缩宽度和深度，显著降低计算量同时保持生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频扩散Transformer计算开销巨大，现有方法采用固定的剪枝架构，无法根据输入内容和去噪阶段自适应调整计算量。

Method: 宽度上：根据注意力头在空间和时间上的专业化角色进行重要性评分，防止剪枝过早移除运动关键的时间头；深度上：训练轻量级路由器，根据去噪时间步和视觉内容动态选择每步执行的模块，实现输入自适应的计算分配。

Result: 在Wan2.1-14B上的图像到视频和文本到视频生成实验中，PARE在VBench上显著降低了每步计算量，同时保持了视频质量，并能与步骤蒸馏结合进一步加速。

Conclusion: PARE提出了一种联合剪枝宽度和深度的自适应框架，通过感知注意力头角色和动态路由实现了高效的视频生成，具有较好的普适性和可组合性。

Abstract: Video Diffusion Transformers (DiTs) generate high-quality videos but demand substantial compute due to wide blocks, deep architectures, and iterative sampling. Recent methods reduce cost by compressing width, depth, or sampling steps, but typically commit to a fixed architecture that cannot adapt to individual inputs or denoising stages. We propose PARE (Pruning and Adaptive Routing for Efficient video generation), which jointly compresses width and depth with structure-aware pruning and input-adaptive routing. For width, we observe that attention heads specialize into spatial and temporal roles, and design importance scoring that accounts for this distinction to prevent motion-critical temporal heads from being pruned prematurely. For depth, we train a lightweight router conditioned on denoising timestep and visual content to dynamically select which blocks to execute at each step, enabling per-input compute adaptation rather than static block removal. A progressive pipeline first recovers width-pruned quality via distillation, then jointly optimizes the student and router to decouple the two learning objectives. Experiments on Wan2.1-14B for both image-to-video and text-to-video generation show that PARE substantially reduces per-step computation while preserving quality across VBench dimensions, and composes with step distillation for further acceleration.

</details>


### [96] [Towards Controllable Image Generation through Representation-Conditioned Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27343)
*Nithesh Chandher Karthikeyan,Jonas Unger,Gabriel Eilertsen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文探讨利用预训练自监督模型的表示来条件化扩散模型，无需大量标注数据即可提升无条件图像生成质量，并实现可控生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型虽在图像生成与编辑中表现优异，但传统的条件机制（如文本提示或语义图）依赖大规模标注数据集，成本高昂且受限，因此探索无需标注的自监督条件化方法成为关键。

Method: 利用预训练自监督模型提取的表示作为扩散模型的条件，通过自条件化机制（self-conditioning）改进无条件生成质量，并通过识别表示空间中的变化方向来控制生成过程。

Result: 该方法在无需标注数据的情况下提升了无条件图像生成质量，且条件表示空间展现出平滑性与解耦性（smoothness and disentanglement）等优良特性。

Conclusion: 自监督条件化为扩散模型提供了一种减少标注依赖、同时增强可控性的有效途径，初步验证了其在表示空间中进行生成控制的可能性。

Abstract: Diffusion models have emerged as powerful tools for high-quality image generation and editing, but guiding these models to produce specific outputs remains a challenge. Conventional approaches rely on conditioning mechanisms, such as text prompts or semantic maps, which require extensively annotated datasets. In this preliminary work, we explore diffusion models conditioned on representations from a pre-trained self-supervised model. The self-conditioning mechanism not only improves the quality of unconditional image generation, but also provides a representation space that can be used to control the generation. We explore this conditioning space by identifying directions of variations, and demonstrate promising properties in terms of smoothness and disentanglement.

</details>


### [97] [When Eyes Betray AI: Social Gaze Consistency as a Semantic Cue for AI-Generated Image Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27348)
*Kim Jihyeon,Sohee Kim,Soosan Lee,Souhwan Jung,James Matthew Rehg,Hyesong Choi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出社会凝视一致性（Social Gaze Consistency）作为高维语义线索用于检测生成图像，通过头部-眼睛对齐、注视方向等交互级语义特征，在多个数据集和架构上显著提升检测性能，并揭示了跨生成器迁移的机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成模型已大幅弥合低层伪影差距（像素指纹、频域异常等），尤其是在人脸中心和局部编辑场景中，操纵区域小且被真实内容包围，传统检测手段失效。因此需要探索更高层次的语义线索。

Method: (i) 构建区域特异性扰动诊断数据集，通过严格配对分组防止生成器指纹记忆；(ii) 提出块组合字幕监督（Block-Compositional Caption Supervision），固定5块推理骨架跨越1250个宏观组合字幕；(iii) 跨架构验证（FakeVLM和Effort），证明该线索与骨干无关。

Result: FakeVLM在COCOAI Interaction子集上平衡准确率提升3.7个百分点（67.8→71.5），在Person子集上提升1.3个百分点（83.0→84.3），Effort骨干也有一致提升。真假类召回率同步上升，排除"全部预测为假"的伪影。

Conclusion: 社会凝视一致性是一种被低估的跨生成器检测线索，单生成器训练即可迁移至多生成器场景，原因包括配对编辑捷径阻断、难易度迁移、CLIP先验保持以及扩散模型在眼周结构上的共享谱弱点。

Abstract: Recent generative models have largely closed the gap on low-level artifacts - pixel fingerprints, frequency anomalies, upsampling traces - particularly in person-centric and partial-edit settings where the manipulated region is small and surrounded by photometrically authentic content. We introduce Social Gaze Consistency, a high-level semantic cue defined as the mutual coherence of gaze direction, head-eye alignment, and pupil placement between interacting individuals, and show that it constitutes a previously underutilized detection axis orthogonal to existing low-level paradigms. We instantiate this insight through three coupled mechanisms: (i) a controlled diagnostic dataset with region-specific perturbations of gaze-consistent imagery, where strict pair-level grouping forecloses generator-fingerprint memorization as an optimization-time shortcut rather than relying on augmentation; (ii) Block-Compositional Caption Supervision, which holds a single 5-block reasoning skeleton invariant across 1,250 macro-combined captions, decoupling reasoning consistency from surface diversity; (iii) Cross-architecture validation showing the same supervision improves a vision-language backbone (FakeVLM) by +3.7 pp on the COCOAI Interaction subset (balanced accuracy 67.8 -> 71.5) and +1.3 pp on the COCOAI Person subset (83.0 -> 84.3), with consistent gains on a vision-only backbone (Effort), evidencing a backbone-agnostic cue. Real- and fake-class recalls rise simultaneously, ruling out a "predict-all-fake" artifact. A four-step mechanistic account - paired-edit shortcut blocking, hard-to-easy difficulty transfer, CLIP prior preservation, and diffusion-family shared spectral weakness in periocular structure - explains why training on a single inpainter (FLUX.1-Fill) transfers to multi-generator suites. We will release the code upon acceptance to facilitate reproducibility.

</details>


### [98] [LocateAnything: Fast and High-Quality Vision-Language Grounding with Parallel Box Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27365)
*Shihao Wang,Shilong Liu,Yuanguo Kuang,Xinyu Wei,Yangzhou Liu,Zhiqi Li,Yunze Man,Guo Chen,Andrew Tao,Guilin Liu,Jan Kautz,Lei Zhang,Zhiding Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LocateAnything提出了一种基于并行框解码（PBD）的统一生成式视觉定位与检测框架，通过单步解码几何元素（如边界框和点）保持几何一致性，配合超1.38亿训练样本的大规模数据集，在提升解码吞吐量的同时提高了定位精度，实现了速度-精度前沿的突破。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM将视觉定位和检测建模为坐标token序列生成问题，各token独立学习解码，这种逐token解码方式与边界框的耦合几何结构不匹配，且严格顺序生成造成推理瓶颈。

Method: 提出并行框解码（PBD），将边界框和点等几何元素作为原子单元单步解码；同时构建包含超过1.38亿训练样本的大规模数据集LocateAnything-Data，以增加数据多样性。

Result: PBD显著提高了解码吞吐量和定位精度，在不同基准测试中均提升了高IoU定位质量，推进了速度-精度前沿。

Conclusion: 并行框解码与大规模训练数据相辅相成，能够实现高效且精确的统一视觉定位与检测。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) commonly formulate visual grounding and detection as a coordinate-token generation problem, serializing each 2D box into multiple 1D tokens that are learned and decoded largely independently. This token-by-token decoding mismatches the coupled structure of box geometry and creates a practical inference bottleneck due to strictly sequential generation. We introduce LocateAnything, a unified generative grounding and detection framework based on Parallel Box Decoding (PBD). By decoding geometric elements such as bounding boxes and points as atomic units in a single step, LocateAnything preserves intra-box geometric coherence and unlocks substantial parallelism. We show that PBD improves both decoding throughput and localization accuracy. We further develop a scalable data engine and curate LocateAnything-Data, a large-scale dataset with more than 138 million training samples, substantially increasing data diversity for high-precision localization. Extensive evaluations show that LocateAnything advances the speed-accuracy frontier, achieving significantly higher decoding throughput while improving high-IoU localization quality across diverse benchmarks. The results highlight the complementary benefits of Parallel Box Decoding and large-scale training data in enabling efficient and precise unified visual grounding and detection.

</details>


### [99] [SpatialBench: Is Your Spatial Foundation Model an All-Round Player?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27367)
*Haosong Peng,Hao Li,Jiaqi Chen,Yuhao Pan,Runmao Yao,Yalun Dai,Fushuo Huo,Fangzhou Hong,Zhaoxi Chen,Haozhao Wang,Dingwen Zhang,Ziwei Liu,Wenchao Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SpatialBench，一个跨范式、多领域的空间基础模型基准测试，通过大规模（19个数据集、546个场景）和确定性采样设计，系统评估了41个模型在6种范式、5个任务套件和4种输入密度下的表现。结果显示当前模型并非全能选手，并揭示了全上下文注意力对精度最优、有界记忆策略可扩展长序列、以及领域对齐和数据质量比简单数据规模更关键等洞察。同时引入DA-Next-5M数据集和DA-Next基线模型推动空间表示学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有空间基础模型评估局限于特定领域和特定设计场景，难以衡量其跨任务、跨视角、跨场景领域的真实泛化能力。作者旨在通过一个全面、标准化的基准来回答当前模型是否真正具备“全能”泛化能力这一核心问题。

Method: 构建SpatialBench基准，包含19个数据集、546个场景，覆盖5个空间领域；采用确定性采样设计确保公平比较；评估41个模型涵盖6个范式、5个任务套件和4种输入密度设置；引入DA-Next-5M大规模数据集和DA-Next基线模型。

Result: 当前模型并非全能选手；全上下文注意力机制在精度上最优，有界记忆策略在长序列可扩展性上表现突出；在具身和以自我为中心的挑战性任务中，严格的领域对齐和高数据质量远比简单扩大数据集规模更重要。

Conclusion: 现有的空间基础模型在泛化能力上仍有显著不足，未来需要更关注领域对齐和数据质量，而非单纯追求数据规模。SpatialBench和DA-Next-5M为未来研究提供了标准和资源。

Abstract: While spatial foundation models have demonstrated impressive performance on standard datasets, a critical question remains: are they truly all-round players capable of generalizing robustly across diverse downstream tasks, arbitrary viewpoints, shifting scene domains, varying input densities, and specific hardware constraints? Answering this overarching question requires a holistic assessment, yet current models are mainly evaluated on specific domains for which they were specifically designed or trained. Such evaluations are intrinsically limited by narrow paradigm coverage, limited scene domains, and arbitrary frame sampling, making it fundamentally difficult to assess their true generalization capabilities. To address this gap, we present SpatialBench, a cross-paradigm, domain-diverse benchmark for spatial foundation models with deterministic sampling. SpatialBench features unprecedented scale and rigorous deterministic design, comprising 19 datasets and 546 scenes across 5 diverse spatial domains. It comprehensively evaluates 41 models across 6 paradigms on 5 task suites under 4 different input density settings. Our extensive evaluation reveals that current models are not yet all-round players, and uncovers crucial insights for future advancement. Specifically, we demonstrate that full-context attention maximizes accuracy while bounded-memory strategies unlock long-sequence scalability. Moreover, our empirical evaluations in challenging embodied and egocentric tasks demonstrate that strict domain alignment and high data quality are far more critical to performance than simple dataset scaling. Furthermore, to address the largest data gap identified in our analysis, we go beyond evaluation by introducing a large-scale dataset, DA-Next-5M, and a strong baseline model, DA-Next, pushing the boundaries of spatial representation learning.

</details>


### [100] [G3T Up! Gravity Aligned Coordinate Frames Simplify Pointmap Processing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27372)
*Bharath Raj Nagoor Kani,Noah Snavely*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出G3T模型，通过在重力对齐的坐标系中预测点云图，利用场景中的强结构先验（如重力方向），提高3D重建的精度和一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有前馈3D重建方法（如VGGT）使用相机中心坐标系，但该坐标系并非最优。重力对齐坐标系在不同视角间共享垂直轴，可减少点云图之间对齐所需的旋转自由度，从而提升重建效果。

Method: 提出Gravity Grounded Geometry Transformer (G3T)，从现有模型微调而来，在重力对齐的3D数据上训练，预测直立点云图和相机到重力的姿态。同时提出G3T-Long，一种基于子图的增量式3D重建管线，利用直立坐标系降低的旋转自由度提升重建精度。

Result: G3T能够生成高度准确的重力感知预测，G3T-Long在增量式3D重建中显著提高了重建精度。

Conclusion: 采用重力对齐的坐标系替代相机中心坐标系，能够利用场景的结构先验，有效提升前馈3D重建方法的性能。

Abstract: Modern feed-forward 3D reconstruction methods like VGGT predict pixel-aligned pointmaps in camera-centric coordinate frames. However, this choice of coordinate frame is not always optimal. We propose instead to predict pointmaps in upright, gravity-aligned frames that exploit strong structural cues present in many real-world scenes. Unlike camera-centric frames, gravity-aligned frames share a common vertical axis across viewpoints, reducing the rotational degrees of freedom needed to relate pointmaps to one another. To this end, we introduce the Gravity Grounded Geometry Transformer (G3T), fine-tuned from existing models on gravity-aligned 3D data. G3T produces highly accurate gravity-aware predictions, including upright pointmaps and camera-to-gravity poses. We further introduce G3T-Long, a submap-based incremental 3D reconstruction pipeline that leverages the reduced rotational degrees of freedom afforded by upright frames to achieve significantly improved reconstruction accuracy.

</details>


<div id='cs.MM'></div>

# cs.MM [[Back]](#toc)

### [101] [Reproducibility Companion Paper: Swarical: An Integrated Hierarchical Approach to Localizing Flying Light Specks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26313)
*Hamed Alimohammadzadeh,Shahram Ghandeharizadeh,Federico Cunico,Joshua Springer*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: 本文提供了ACM Multimedia 2024论文"Swarical"的可重复实验指南，该技术通过分层方法实现微型无人机（FLS）的精准定位与形状照明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实现微型无人机集群对复杂2D/3D形状的精准定位与高效照明。

Method: 提出Swarical分层定位技术，包含离线规划器（根据传感器规格转换网格文件）和在线定位执行模块。

Result: 在线定位技术被报告为快速且高精度。

Conclusion: 提供可复现实验的工件与操作指南，验证Swarical方法的有效性。

Abstract: This companion paper provides artifacts and instructions on replicating the experiments in the ACM Multimedia 2024 paper entitled "Swarical: An Integrated Hierarchical Approach to Localizing Flying Light Specks." Swarm-based hierarchical, Swarical, is a localization technique that enables miniature drones, Flying Light Specks (FLSs), to accurately and efficiently localize and illuminate complex 2D and 3D shapes. It consists of two components, an offline planner and an online localization technique that executes on an FLS. The offline planner uses the FLS sensor specification for positioning to convert mesh files into swarms of FLSs. Some FLSs are dark and used only for localization. We reported the online localization technique to be fast and highly accurate. We describe how to reproduce this finding using our artifacts.

</details>


### [102] [Can We Hear from Events? Generating Speech from Event Camera](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26672)
*Jingping Fang,Lin Chen,Chenyang Xu,Tong Zhao,Weidong Cai,Xiaoming Chen*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: 本文提出EventSpeech，一种利用神经形态事件相机（微秒级精度）替代传统RGB相机进行文本驱动情感语音生成的新框架，解决了传统方法因曝光时间导致的时域粒度不匹配问题，在情感保留和运动模糊抵抗上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统RGB相机固定曝光时间会模糊高频发音瞬态特征，导致情感语音生成的时域粒度不匹配，限制了表达性语音生成的上限。

Method: 提出EventSpeech框架，包含专用事件编码器（Event Encoder）处理稀疏神经形态事件，多尺度音频编码器（含层级小波上下文器HWC），以及双向对齐机制来同步语言内容、视觉动态与密集声学特征。同时构建了首个基准数据集EVT-SPK（含合成数据和真实神经形态硬件采集数据）。

Result: EventSpeech在情感保留和运动模糊抵抗方面显著优于当前基线方法，建立了多模态语音生成的新范式。

Conclusion: 神经形态事件信号因其微秒级精度天然匹配声学波形动态，为情感语音生成提供了突破传统RGB限制的新路径，EventSpeech验证了该范式的有效性。

Abstract: Traditional RGB-based speech generation faces Temporal Granularity Mismatch since fixed camera exposure times inevitably blur the high-frequency articulatory transients essential for rendering emotional speech. To break this ceiling, we propose EventSpeech as a novel text-conditioned framework pioneering the use of neuromorphic events for expressive speech generation, since these microsecond-precise events naturally align with acoustic waveform dynamics. Our architecture integrates a dedicated Event Encoder to model sparse neuromorphic events alongside a multi-scale Audio Encoder featuring a Hierarchical Wavelet Contextualizer (HWC). A bidirectional alignment mechanism seamlessly synchronizes linguistic content and visual dynamics with dense acoustic features. Furthermore, we construct EVT-SPK as the first benchmark comprising large-scale synthetic data and real-world recordings from specialized neuromorphic hardware. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that EventSpeech significantly outperforms current baselines by preserving fine-grained emotions and resisting motion blur to establish a new paradigm for multimodal speech generation. Code and demo are available at https://xrfang-0102.github.io/EventSpeechWeb/.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [103] [AirCast-SR: A Foundation Model for Kilometer-Scale Atmospheric Super-Resolution via Latent Consistency Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26130)
*Somnath Luitel,Manmeet Singh,Joshua Durkee,Abdullah Al Fahad,Naveen Sudharsan,Prabhjot Singh,Cenlin He,Harsh Kamath,Zong-Liang Yang,Krishnagopal Halder,Sandeep Juneja,Parthasarathi Mukhopadhyay,Saptarishi Dhanuka,Amit Kumar Srivastava*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出EarthMind-SR，一种基于扩散模型的大气超分辨率基础模型，可将全球AI天气预报从0.25度（~28公里）降尺度至1公里分辨率，在67小时预报范围内实现近乎零偏差，并展示零样本全球迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统数值天气预报在公里级尺度上计算成本过高，限制了能源、农业和灾害管理等应用领域对细粒度时空细节的获取。

Method: 采用三维U-Net在潜在一致性模型（LCM）扩散框架中进行条件生成，以GraphCast预报为输入、NOAA的AORC数据为目标，在美国本土进行基于图块的训练。

Result: 所有变量和预报时次接近零偏差，径向功率谱密度分析显示在10-100公里波长范围内保留了精细大气结构，并在印度和德国实现了零样本全球迁移。

Conclusion: 作为开放权重基础模型，EarthMind-SR建立了公里级AI天气预报的新范式，为区域微调、蒸馏以及在气候服务和灾害预报中的应用提供了平台。

Abstract: Operational weather prediction at kilometer scales remains computationally prohibitive for traditional numerical weather prediction (NWP) models, limiting forecast access for applications in energy, agriculture, and disaster management that require fine-grained spatiotemporal detail. Here we introduce AirCast-SR, a foundation model for atmospheric super-resolution that downscales global AI weather forecasts from 0.25 degree (~28 km) to 1 km horizontal resolution at hourly temporal resolution, producing 67-hour forecasts of eight coupled surface variables simultaneously. EarthMind-SR employs a three-dimensional U-Net conditioned within a Latent Consistency Model (LCM) diffusion framework, trained on patch-based samples over the contiguous United States (CONUS) using GraphCast forecasts as input and NOAA's Analysis of Record for Calibration (AORC) as the target. The model achieves near-zero bias across all variables and lead times, and its radial power spectral density analysis demonstrates preservation of fine-scale atmospheric structure at wavelengths of 10 km to 100 km where coarser models lose spectral power. We validate EarthMind-SR across three CONUS case studies spanning winter, summer, and spring seasons, and demonstrate zero-shot global transferability over India and Germany using independent surface station observations without any retraining or fine-tuning. As an open-weights foundation model, EarthMind-SR establishes a new paradigm for kilometer-scale AI weather prediction and provides a platform for regional fine-tuning, distillation, and downstream applications in climate services and hazard forecasting.

</details>


### [104] [SilIF: Silhouette-Augmented Isolation Forest for Unsupervised Transaction Fraud Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26135)
*Venkatakrishnan Gopalakrishnan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SilIF 是一种对孤立森林 (Isolation Forest) 的增强方法，通过在森林树结构形成的表示空间中对每个点的路径长度指纹进行聚类并计算轮廓分数，将轮廓信号与基础 IF 分数结合。在 IEEE-CIS 欺诈检测基准上，SilIF 在 AUC-PR 上平均提升 0.0080，但在合成信用卡数据集上无改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无监督异常检测在标签稀疏的交易欺诈检测中广泛应用，孤立森林虽流行但存在改进空间，因此提出一种基于轮廓系数的增强方法以提升检测效果。

Method: SilIF 提取每个点在每棵树上的路径长度形成"指纹"向量，对其进行聚类得到结构分组，计算轮廓分数衡量点与其所属组及最近邻组的匹配程度，再通过超参数 alpha 将轮廓信号与基础 IF 分数融合。

Result: 在 IEEE-CIS 基准上（约59万交易，3.5%欺诈率），SilIF（alpha=1.0）在五次实验平均 AUC-PR 上比原始 IF 提升 0.0080，五次均胜出（配对t检验 p=0.046）；在合成信用卡数据集上无改进。

Conclusion: SilIF 是一种可调参、易部署的孤立森林增强方法，论文诚实报告了其在某些场景有效、某些场景无效的条件差异。

Abstract: Unsupervised anomaly detection is widely used in transaction fraud detection where labels are scarce. Isolation Forest (IF) is among the most popular classical methods due to its scalability and ease of deployment. We propose SilIF, an augmentation of Isolation Forest that adds a silhouette-based scoring layer computed in a representation space induced by the trees of the forest. For each point, we extract a vector of per-tree path lengths, cluster these "fingerprints" into structural groups, and compute a silhouette score that measures how well the point fits its assigned group versus the nearest alternative. The silhouette signal is combined with the base IF score via a single hyperparameter alpha. On the IEEE-CIS Fraud Detection benchmark (~590K transactions, 3.5% fraud), SilIF with alpha=1.0 improves over plain Isolation Forest by +0.0080 AUC-PR on average across five seeds, with SilIF winning on all five seeds (paired t-test p=0.046). We also report results on a synthetic credit-card dataset (Sparkov) where the silhouette augmentation does not improve over plain IF, and we characterize the conditions that distinguish the two outcomes. The paper presents SilIF as a tunable, easy-to-deploy enhancement to Isolation Forest with honest reporting of when it helps and when it does not. Code at https://github.com/venkat15vk/silif-anomaly-detection.

</details>


### [105] [On the Push-Based Asynchronous Federated Learning: A Bias-Correction Aggregation Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26162)
*Jiahui Bai,Hai Dong,A. K. Qin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出PushCen-ADFL框架，通过质心表示空间将通信、聚合和局部稳定耦合，在异步去中心化联邦学习中减少通信开销80%以上并提升准确率6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异步去中心化联邦学习面临通信开销大、拓扑不对称导致的聚合偏差以及非IID数据造成的模型漂移等问题，现有方法难以同时解决这些挑战。

Method: PushCen-ADFL框架：(1) 客户端交换质心形式的消息；(2) 采用保平均的push-sum混合纠正聚合偏差；(3) 轻量级质心正则化缓解异构性和陈旧性导致的漂移；(4) 有界发送端去重缓冲区提升对不规则异步到达的鲁棒性。

Result: 在视觉数据集上，PushCen-ADFL在数据异质性下准确率提升高达6%，每次推送通信成本降低超80%，实现了良好的准确率-通信权衡。

Conclusion: PushCen-ADFL通过质心空间的闭环设计有效解决了ADFL中的通信效率与训练稳定性问题，适用于大规模异构系统。

Abstract: Asynchronous decentralized federated learning (ADFL) eliminates central coordination and global synchronization, making it attractive for large-scale and heterogeneous systems. However, frequent peer-to-peer communication, asynchronous updates on directed topologies, and non-IID data jointly lead to excessive communication overhead, biased aggregation and severe model drift. We propose PushCen-ADFL, a communication-efficient ADFL framework that enables stable training under asymmetric communication and delayed client participation. PushCen-ADFL couples communication, aggregation, and local stabilization in a shared centroid representation space, forming a closed loop between compression and optimization. Clients exchange centroid-form messages, apply average-preserving push-sum mixing to correct aggregation bias, and use a lightweight centroid regularization anchored in the same centroid space to mitigate drift under heterogeneity and staleness. A bounded, sender-deduplicated buffer further improves robustness under irregular asynchronous arrivals. Experiments on vision datasets demonstrate that PushCen-ADFL improves accuracy under data heterogeneity by up to 6\% while reducing per-push communication cost by more than 80\%, achieving a favorable accuracy-communication trade-off.

</details>


### [106] [When Rule Violations Are Rare: Chimera Training for Logical Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26171)
*Alejandro Ascarate,Leo Lebrat,Rodrigo Santa Cruz,Clinton Fookes,Olivier Salvado*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于逻辑规则的神经规则评估器，通过特征级子树拼接的"嵌合体训练"策略生成反事实样本，在概念约束违反的异常检测任务中提升组合规则层面的异常检测AUC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际异常不仅是罕见输入，更是对语义约束（如对象共现、动作前提、时序规律）的违反。训练时约束违规样本稀缺或缺失，如何仅从正常样本中学习检测规则违反成为挑战。

Method: 1）将每个逻辑约束编译为有向无环图，内部逻辑算子采用特征感知的子树MLP门控；2）提出嵌合体训练：在不同样本间拼接子树特征，每个操作数保留原样本的真值标签，通过逻辑运算符合成目标，生成反事实训练样本。

Result: 在CLEVRER、OpenImages、VidOR数据集上，该方法在规则级异常检测AUROC上超越独立事件基准和同图语义训练基线，尤其在组合规则和关系规则上优势显著。

Conclusion: 所提方法能有效检测语义约束违反，输出标量异常分数与规则级归因，无需真实异常图像即可获得监督信号。

Abstract: Many practical anomalies are not merely rare inputs, but violations of semantic constraints: objects co-occur in structured ways, actions imply preconditions, and events satisfy temporal or relational regularities. We study anomaly detection in this setting, where constraints are given as logical rules over learned visual concepts, but real rule violations are rare or absent during training. We propose a neural rule evaluator that compiles each constraint into a directed acyclic graph and learns feature-aware subtree MLP gates for its internal logical operators. Each gate maps child features and edge-level negations to a parent representation and a rule-satisfaction probability, with intermediate supervision obtained from exact Boolean propagation over ground-truth concept labels. The key difficulty is that same-image training data often provide insufficient coverage of informative truth configurations and also allow shortcut solutions. To address this, we introduce chimera training: an operand-level counterfactual construction at the feature level. Instead of mixing input images, we concatenate subtree features from different samples; each operand keeps the hard truth label of the sample it came from, and the chimera target is obtained by applying the node's logical operator to those inherited labels. This supplies supervised logical counterexamples without requiring real anomalous images. Across CLEVRER, OpenImages, and VidOR, the resulting evaluator improves rule-level anomaly AUROC over independent-events and same-image semantic-training baselines, especially for compositional and relational rules. The method yields both scalar anomaly scores and rule-level attributions.

</details>


### [107] [InfoQuant: Shaping Activation Distributions for Low-Bit LLM Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26175)
*Ke Li,Dong An,Xiaoling Zang,Can Ye,Liang Xie,Qibo Qiu,Chen Shen,Xiaofei He,Wenxiao Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: InfoQuant是一种免训练的后量化方法，通过峰值抑制正交变换（PSOT）将激活分布重塑为更适合低比特量化的形式，在W4A4KV4设置下平均保留97%的浮点精度，并在LLaMA-2 13B上将性能差距较先前最优方法缩小42%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低比特激活量化是高效部署大语言模型的主要瓶颈，现有方法虽然抑制峰值、平衡通道或最小化重构误差，但很少明确什么样的激活分布才易于量化，导致激活值数值上更平滑但量化误差仍然很大。

Method: 从信息论角度分析量化误差，提出量化友好的激活应同时具有较小的数值范围和足够的分散度；据此设计免训练的InfoQuant方法，采用峰值抑制正交变换（PSOT）重塑激活分布，并引入自适应异常token选择以提升优化鲁棒性。

Result: 在多个LLM家族上，InfoQuant一致优于先前的PTQ和端到端训练基线。在W4A4KV4设置下平均保留97%的浮点精度，将LLaMA-2 13B的性能差距较之前最优方法缩小42%。

Conclusion: 从信息论角度重新审视激活变换问题，明确了量化友好分布的关键属性，所提出的InfoQuant方法有效且无需训练，为LLM低比特量化部署提供了新思路。

Abstract: Low-bit activation quantization remains a major bottleneck in efficient large language model (LLM) deployment. The difficulty is not only that activations contain outliers, but that their distributions are often poorly matched to a low-bit uniform quantizer. Existing post-training quantization (PTQ) methods suppress peaks, balance channels, or minimize reconstruction error, yet they rarely specify what activation distribution is actually easy to discretize. As a result, activations may appear numerically smoother while still incurring large quantization error because the quantization range remains wide or most values collapse into a few levels near the mean. We recast activation transformation as quantizer-facing distribution design and analyze quantization error from an information-theoretic perspective. Our analysis shows that quantization-friendly activations should jointly have a smaller numerical range and sufficient dispersion within that range. Guided by this analysis, we propose InfoQuant, a train-free method that employs Peak Suppression Orthogonal Transformation (PSOT) to shape activations into more quantization-friendly distributions. We further introduce adaptive outlier-token selection to improve the robustness of PSOT during optimization. Across multiple LLM families, InfoQuant consistently outperforms prior PTQ and end-to-end training baselines. Under W4A4KV4, it preserves 97% of floating-point accuracy on average and reduces the LLaMA-2 13B performance gap by 42% over the previous state of the art. Code is available at [https://github.com/LLIKKE/InfoQuant](https://github.com/LLIKKE/InfoQuant)

</details>


### [108] [GAC: Noise-Aware Adaptive Mixing for Hybrid SFT-RL Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26184)
*Yuelin Hu,Zhenbo Yu,Zhengxue Cheng,Wei Liu,Li Song*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出GAC，一种基于梯度方差和信号差异估计的自适应混合权重方法，用于改进后训练中的固定混合策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的混合后训练（SFT+RL）使用固定的混合比例，无法适应两种训练信号相对噪声随时间变化的动态情况。

Method: GAC通过在线估计梯度方差和两训练信号间的不一致性，得到自适应混合权重；并添加平滑、先验引导和有界更新，复用已有训练张量。

Result: 在数学、代码、科学和逻辑基准测试上，GAC一致优于固定和基于规则的基线，模型越大增益越明显，训练开销低于1%。

Conclusion: GAC是一种轻量、自适应的混合权重控制器，能有效提升混合后训练性能。

Abstract: Hybrid post-training usually combines supervised fine-tuning and reinforcement learning, but fixed mixing schedules cannot adapt when the relative noise of the two signals changes over time. We propose GAC, a noise-aware controller that derives an adaptive mixing weight from online estimates of gradient variance and disagreement between the two training signals. The method adds smoothing, prior guidance, and bounded updates while reusing existing training tensors. Experiments on math, code, science, and logic benchmarks show that GAC consistently improves hybrid post-training over strong fixed and rule-based baselines, with larger gains at larger model scales and less than 1% training overhead.

</details>


### [109] [Max-Window Scale Estimation for Near-Lossless HiF8 W8A8 Quantization-Aware Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26189)
*Yingying Cheng,Jinquan Shi,Li Zhou,Zhiyang He,Zhaoyi Sun,Fan Zhang,Jie Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了1B参数大模型在HiF8 W8A8量化感知训练中的两种隐性失败模式：amax饱和与灾难性遗忘，并提出了保守的DTS策略和BF16预热训练方法，在MMLU等多项基准上实现了与BF16基线几乎持平的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低比特浮点量化感知训练（QAT）在部署大语言模型时效率高，但存在标准训练指标无法察觉的隐性失败模式，亟需系统研究并解决这些问题。

Method: 通过8组控制实验，解耦了两种正交失败模式：amax饱和（延迟缩放估计导致前向裁剪破坏知识表征）和灾难性遗忘（激进学习率覆盖预训练常识）。提出64步历史窗口的保守max算法DTS策略，以及500步BF16预热+lr=10^{-5}的QAT方案。

Result: 最终配置在MMLU下降0.43%，HellaSwag下降0.58%，ARC-Challenge下降0.22%，训练损失APE仅0.11%。

Conclusion: 两种修复方案（保守DTS与BF16预热+低学习率）是必要且充分的，有效解决了QAT中不可检测的隐性失败模式。

Abstract: Quantization-aware training (QAT) with low-bit floating-point formats enables efficient LLM deployment, yet introduces subtle failure modes invisible to standard training metrics. We present a systematic study of HiF8 W8A8 QAT for OpenPangu-Embedded-1B through the lens of Delayed Tensor Scaling (DTS). Across eight controlled experiments, we identify and disentangle two orthogonal failure modes: (i)amax saturation, where delayed scale estimates silently corrupt knowledge-sensitive representations via forward-pass clipping, and (ii)catastrophic forgetting, where an aggressive learning rate overwrites pretrained commonsense knowledge independently of quantization. Neither is detectable from training loss alone. We address amax saturation with a conservative max-algorithm DTS strategy over a 64-step history window, and mitigate forgetting via a 500-step BF16 warmup followed by QAT at lr=10^{-5}. Both fixes are necessary and sufficient: our final configuration achieves 0.43% MMLU drop, 0.58% HellaSwag drop, and 0.22% ARC-Challenge drop versus a matched BF16 baseline, with a training loss APE of only 0.11% over 10,000 steps.

</details>


### [110] [Modeling Dynamic Mixtures of Time-Delay Systems from Streaming Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26191)
*Ren Fujiwara,Yasuko Matsubara,Yasushi Sakurai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DelayMix，一种将流式时间序列视为动态混合时滞系统的在线建模框架，通过固定长度的马尔可夫参数张量总结历史模式，实现快速适应环境变化并降低内存消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列数据流中存在系统突变（如环境变化或输入延迟变化），导致模型性能下降；同时使用多个小模型应对不同模式时，在准确性、鲁棒性和内存使用之间存在权衡。

Method: 将流式时间序列建模为动态混合时滞系统，构建基于马尔可夫参数序列的摘要系统张量，捕捉系统动态行为和延迟特征，必要时使用张量分解算法提取相关历史模型并选择最佳匹配当前模式的系统。

Result: 在真实数据集上，DelayMix在预测准确性和延迟适应速度上持续优于其他方法，尤其在高非平稳数据上表现突出。

Conclusion: 该方法能快速适应环境变化，计算高效，在保持鲁棒性的同时降低内存使用，适用于非平稳时间序列流场景。

Abstract: This research addresses the problem of adaptive modeling in time-series data streams with clear input-output relationships. This problem is challenging because rapid system changes (regime shifts) caused by environmental factors or input delay changes degrade model performance, and the trade-off among accuracy, robustness, and memory usage arises when using multiple small models for each time-series pattern. To address these issues, this paper presents an online framework/method that treats streaming time series as dynamic mixtures of time-delay systems. This framework maintains robustness of model tracking and reduces memory usage by summarizing past regimes using a fixed-length representation that captures both the system dynamics and input-output delays. Concretely, this approach constructs a summary system tensor using the system's Markov parameter series, capturing both dynamic behavior and delay characteristics. If necessary, a tensor decomposition algorithm extracts relevant past models from the tensor and helps select the system that best fits the current regime. This method enables rapid adaptation to environmental changes and is computationally efficient. Tests on real datasets show that DelayMix consistently outperforms other methods, achieving superior forecast accuracy and faster adaptation to delays, especially for highly non-stationary data.

</details>


### [111] [Co-folding model guided by structural proteomics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26192)
*Alon Shtrikman,Nitzan Simchi,Michal Ran Shchory,Sagie Brodsky,Eran Seger,Kirill Pevzner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AIMS-Fold是一个推理时引导扩散框架，通过整合XL-MS和HDX-MS实验数据与预训练扩散模型的生物物理先验，显著提升了蛋白质复合物构象状态预测的准确性，在诱导邻近靶标上优于纯计算模型Boltz-2。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有蛋白质结构生成模型虽能预测静态结构，但无法捕捉蛋白质复合物的正确构象状态，这对抗体和PROTAC等诱导邻近药物设计至关重要。结构蛋白质组学数据虽提供有价值信息，但整合稀疏异质测量数据到生成模型中仍是一个未解决的挑战。

Method: 提出AIMS-Fold框架，在推理阶段通过可微物理势函数（基于XL-MS空间约束和HDX-MS溶剂可及性图谱）主动引导扩散生成轨迹，将实验数据整合到预训练扩散模型中。

Result: 该方法单独使用每种结构数据都能提升预测精度，二者整合产生协同效应。在具有挑战性的诱导邻近靶标上，AIMS-Fold的准确率高于无实验引导的SOTA模型Boltz-2。

Conclusion: 该框架建立了一种强大的整合计算途径，可有效用于诱导邻近药物的基于结构的药物设计。

Abstract: Protein structure generative models excel at predicting single protein static structures from sequence, but routinely fail to capture the correct conformational state of protein complexes, critical for protein design and induced proximity modalities such as antibodies and PROTACs. While structural proteomics techniques like Cross-Linking Mass Spectrometry (XL-MS) and Hydrogen-Deuterium Exchange (HDX-MS) offer valuable spatial and dynamic insights, integrating these sparse, heterogeneous measurements into these models remains an open challenge. Here, we bridge this gap by combining structural proteomics data with the rich biophysical priors learned by pretrained diffusion models. We introduce AIMS-Fold, an inference-time guided-diffusion framework that actively steers the generative sampling trajectory using differentiable physical potentials derived from XL-MS spatial restraints and HDX-MS solvent accessibility profiles. We demonstrate that these structural methods individually enhance predictive accuracy, and their integration yields synergistic improvement. Crucially, by leveraging these experimental restraints, AIMS-Fold achieves higher accuracy on challenging induced proximity targets than purely computational, unguided state-of-the-art models like Boltz-2. This establishes our framework as a powerful, integrative computational approach for the structure based drug design of induced proximity drugs. Evaluation code will be made publicly available upon publication.

</details>


### [112] [Bridging Classification and Reconstruction: Cooperative Time Series Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26193)
*Qideng Tang,Dai Chaofan,Wubin Ma,Yahui Wu,Haohao Zhou,Tao Zhang,Huan Li,Dalin Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CoAD框架，通过统一分类（OE）与重建（MAE）两种范式，利用概率引导的软掩码机制实现协同检测，在时间序列异常检测中显著优于现有方法，且轻量高效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习方法难以检测微妙和长期异常；OE方法泛化能力差，MAE方法存在掩码对齐问题。亟需一种能结合两者优势、弥补各自缺陷的统一框架。

Method: 提出CoAD框架：分类模块生成概率引导的软掩码，指导重建模块；重建模块反过来缓解分类模块的泛化问题。分类模块还针对分类粒度和频率信息缺失进行了专门设计。

Result: 在高质量基准数据集上，CoAD显著优于最先进的深度学习和传统数据挖掘方法，且模型轻量、速度远超现有SOTA方法。

Conclusion: CoAD验证了分类与重建双范式协同的有效性，展示了深度学习在TSAD中的潜力，同时具备实际应用价值。

Abstract: Time series anomaly detection (TSAD) has long been a hot research topic in data mining due to its various applications. Recent studies challenge the effectiveness of popular deep learning methods for TSAD, suggesting their failure in detecting subtle and prolonged anomalies. Outlier Exposure (OE) and Masked Autoencoder (MAE) emerge as two promising paradigms (classification and reconstruction) for solving the above problems. However, OE-based methods are constrained by poor generalization, while MAE-based methods are limited by masking misalignment issues. To address these limitations, this paper proposes a novel framework, CoAD, which unifies the two paradigms to leverage their complementary strengths while mitigating their respective weaknesses. In this framework, the classification module generates probability-informed soft masks for the reconstruction module, which in turn alleviates the generalization problem of the classification module. This cooperative design enables CoAD to effectively detect subtle and complex anomalies that are often overlooked by existing methods. Additionally, the classification module is carefully designed to resolve issues related to improper classification granularity and the neglect of frequency information. Extensive experiments on high-quality benchmark datasets, conducted under rigorous evaluation protocols, demonstrate that CoAD significantly outperforms both state-of-the-art deep learning and traditional data mining methods, highlighting the potential of deep learning in TSAD. Moreover, CoAD is lightweight and substantially faster than existing SOTA methods, demonstrating its practical value for large-scale, real-time applications.

</details>


### [113] [On the Role of Inductive Bias in Time-Series Pretraining: A Case Study in Learning Generalizable Representations for Clinical Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26194)
*Sharmita Dey,Diego Paez-Granados*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PathoFM是一个面向脊髓损伤病理步态分析的编码器中心Transformer，通过三种互补目标（局部重构、时间连续性、无监督上下文动态）进行预训练，实验发现动态为中心的混合目标能产生最均衡的跨任务迁移效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床时间序列学习受限于小规模异质队列和协议漂移，而下游任务涵盖分类和回归，亟需探索何种预训练归纳偏置能实现跨任务类型和跨主体的表征迁移。

Method: 提出PathoFM模型，基于编码器中心Transformer架构，在多变量步态窗口上联合优化三个互补目标：(1)局部重构——重建连续掩码片段以捕捉局部结构；(2)时间连续性——从观测前缀预测掩码中期延续以保持平滑性和因果一致性；(3)无监督上下文动态——通过注意力机制基于主体示例窗口进行支持-查询重构。

Result: 对比各类目标族（分组/对比、基于动态、生成式重构）发现：动态为中心的混合目标产生最均衡的迁移效果——分组目标有利于判别边界但损害连续目标的幅度保真度，纯重构目标保留波形结构但分类表现欠佳。

Conclusion: 结合局部重构与时间连续性，并在可获得示例样本时添加上下文条件，能够产生鲁棒的跨主体泛化表征。

Abstract: Clinical time-series learning is routinely constrained by small, heterogeneous cohorts and protocol drift, while its downstream use spans both classification (e.g., pathology diagnosis) and regression (e.g., temporal forecasting). These constraints make foundation-model pretraining appealing, but raises an important question of which inductive biases should the pretraining objective impose so that representations transfer across task types and subjects. We study this question in pathological gait analysis for spinal cord injury (SCI) via PathoFM, an encoder-centric transformer pretrained on multivariate gait windows with three complementary objectives: Local Completion (reconstruct contiguous masked spans to enforce local structure), Temporal Continuity (predict a masked mid-horizon continuation from an observed prefix to enforce smoothness and causal consistency), and Unsupervised In-Context Dynamics (support-query reconstruction conditioned on subject exemplar windows via attention).
