Empirically comparing objective families (grouping/contrastive, dynamics-based, and generative reconstruction), we find that dynamics-centric mixtures produce the most balanced transfer: grouping objectives favor discriminative margins but can degrade magnitude fidelity needed for continuous targets, whereas reconstruction-only objectives preserve waveform structure but may underperform on classification. Overall, combining local reconstruction with temporal continuity, and adding in-context conditioning when exemplar access is realistic, yields robust subject-generalizing representations.

</details>


### [114] [The Bridge-Garden Dilemma in LLM Distillation: Why Mixing Hard and Soft Labels Works](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26246)
*Guanghui Wang,Kaiwen Lv Kacuila,Zhiyong Yang,Zitai Wang,Jin-Wen Wu,Longtao Huang,Qianqian Xu,Qingming Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 知识蒸馏中混合硬标签和软标签比单独使用任何一种效果更好，原因在于减少了曝光偏差。作者提出Bridge-Garden分解理论，并开发了自适应混合监督方法，性能优于现有基线且训练成本降低9.7倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管软标签理论上包含更丰富的信息，但实验发现混合硬标签和软标签能带来更优的结果，且这种提升不能简单地归因于更好地模仿教师模型。作者旨在揭示这一现象背后的原因并开发更有效的知识蒸馏方法。

Method: 提出Bridge-Garden分解理论：将生成步骤分为"桥梁"（需精确匹配下一个token）和"花园"（可灵活选择）。基于此理论设计了Bridge-Garden混合监督方法，自适应地在硬标签和软标签之间平衡。

Result: 在Qwen、Llama、Gemma、DeepSeek等七个教师-学生模型对及推理和编程基准测试中，该方法优于基于散度的和基于策略的KD基线，同时训练成本降低9.7倍。

Conclusion: 硬标签在"桥梁"步骤避免风险偏差，软标签在"花园"步骤保持多样性，混合策略能有效减少整个序列的曝光偏差。Bridge-Garden方法实现了高效的模型压缩。

Abstract: Knowledge distillation (KD) transfers knowledge from a large teacher model to a smaller student. In language modeling, the student is trained either on tokens sampled from the teacher (hard labels) or the teacher's full next-token distribution (soft labels). Despite soft labels appear strictly richer, we find that mixing hard and soft labels consistently yields better results. Crucially, we show that this gain cannot be explained by closer teacher matching during training. Instead, it comes from reduced exposure bias, the mismatch between training and inference distributions. To explain this phenomenon, we introduce the Bridge-Garden Decomposition theory, which categorizes generation steps into two types: Bridges, where the next token must be exact, and Gardens, where it can be flexible. We show that hard-only KD excels in Bridges by avoiding risky deviations, while soft-only KD preserves diversity in Gardens. A hybrid strategy handles both cases and, as a result, reduces exposure bias across the sequence. Guided by this theory, we develop a family of Bridge-Garden hybrid supervision methods that adaptively balance hard and soft labels. Across a primary suite of seven teacher-student pairs (including Qwen, Llama, Gemma, and DeepSeek) and benchmarks in reasoning and coding, our approach outperforms divergence-based and on-policy KD baselines while reducing training cost by 9.7x, enabling efficient model compression. Code is available at https://github.com/ghwang-s/bridge_garden_hybrid_kd_release.

</details>


### [115] [Quantized Keys Steal Attention: Bias Correction for KV-Cache Compression in Video Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26266)
*Tuna Tuncer,Felix Becker,Thomas Pfeil*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对视频扩散模型中KV缓存量化导致的质量下降问题，提出了一种基于Jensen偏差的注意力校正方法，在几乎零额外开销下恢复量化损失的质量，INT2量化下达到接近BF16的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视频生成中KV缓存成为内存瓶颈，量化虽能减少内存占用但会严重降低视频质量，需要找到一种在压缩内存的同时保持高质量的方法。

Method: 识别出量化导致质量下降的核心机制是Softmax的凸性引起的Jensen偏差（量化噪声使缓存的keys获得过多注意力权重）；推导出逐注意力分数的校正项，利用量化步长和查询向量的范数在线计算，通过二阶泰勒展开使额外计算开销可忽略不计。

Result: 在MAGI-1、SkyReels-V2和HY-WorldPlay三个模型上，INT2量化+校正方法恢复了大部分质量损失，接近BF16精度，且比INT4量化节省50%内存。

Conclusion: 提出的注意力校正方法能够在不增加额外内存的情况下有效消除量化带来的注意力偏差，使得极低位宽量化（INT2）在实际应用中可行。

Abstract: Chunk-wise autoregressive video diffusion models rely on a KV cache of previously generated chunks to avoid redundant computation, but this cache quickly becomes a memory bottleneck as videos grow longer. Methods that quantize the KV cache to low bitwidths reduce memory pressure but degrade video quality. We show that a key driver of this degradation is a systematic bias in attention weights: due to the convexity of the exponential in softmax attention, quantization noise inflates the contribution of cached keys, a phenomenon we call the Jensen bias. This effect causes quantized keys to steal attention mass from the unquantized current chunk. We derive a per-attention-score correction that removes this bias in expectation, computed on the fly from the quantization step sizes of the cached keys and the query norm. Using a second-order Taylor approximation, the additional computational overhead is negligible, and no additional memory is needed alongside the cache. Evaluated on MAGI-1, SkyReels-V2, and HY-WorldPlay at INT2 quantization, our correction recovers most of the quality lost to aggressive quantization, reaching near-BF16 video quality, and can outperform INT4 quantization while using 50% less memory.

</details>


### [116] [Scaling World-Model Reinforcement Learning Through Diffusion Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26282)
*Xiaoyuan Cheng,Wenxuan Yuan,Zhancun Mu,Yuanzhao Zhang,Yiming Yang,Hai Wang,Zhuo Sun,Che Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于扩散策略的模型强化学习方法MBDPO，通过统一搜索与策略优化来解决现有世界模型方法中的结构性错配问题，在多种任务场景下表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于世界模型的强化学习方法在实际规模化应用中存在根本性局限，除了已知的模型偏差和误差累积问题外，还存在搜索与价值学习之间的结构性错配问题：策略改进依赖于非搜索策略诱导的价值函数，导致训练不一致和次优学习。

Method: 提出MBDPO框架，通过扩散策略表示统一搜索与策略优化。将策略优化重构为对潜在世界模型中搜索轨迹的扩散过程，从收集的数据集中提取隐式能量函数来锚定策略，通过优化分数场来缓解错配问题。

Result: 在多任务离线预训练、在线学习、离线到在线微调等多种场景下进行了评估。在离线场景中，通过在大规模数据集上预训练观察其扩展行为，发现随着模型容量增加，性能呈现一致且单调的提升。

Conclusion: MBDPO通过扩散策略统一搜索与优化，有效解决了世界模型方法中的结构性错配问题，释放了世界模型在可扩展策略学习中的潜力。

Abstract: Model-based reinforcement learning (RL) can be effectively supported at scale through the use of world models. However, in practice, scaling such approaches remains fundamentally limited. A commonly recognized challenge is model bias and error compounding, which degrade long-horizon predictions. Beyond these issues, we identify a more critical yet underexplored bottleneck: a structural misalignment between search and value learning in existing world model approaches. In particular, policy improvement often relies on value functions induced by a separate, non-search policy, resulting in training inconsistency and ultimately suboptimal learning. To address this limitation, we propose Model-Based Diffusion Policy Optimization (MBDPO) in world models, a framework that unifies search and policy optimization through diffusion policy representations, thereby unlocking the potential of world models for scalable policy learning. Instead of constructing an explicit planner over a learned world model, we reformulate policy optimization as a diffusion process over searched trajectories in latent world models. In this view, we extract an implicit energy function from the collected dataset that anchors the policy, enabling MBDPO to refine the score field for policy optimization while mitigating misalignment. We evaluate MBDPO across a wide range of settings, including multi-task offline pretraining, online learning, and offline-to-online fine-tuning. In the offline regime, we further investigate its scaling behavior by pretraining on large-scale datasets, observing consistent and monotonic performance gains with increasing model capacity.

</details>


### [117] [Two-Parameter Flows for Learning Population Dynamics of Physical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26285)
*Paul Schwerdtner,Tobias Blickhan,Benjamin Peherstorfer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需轨迹信息、仅利用未标记样本学习高维概率密度随时间演化的方法，通过两参数流实现采样时间与物理时间的解耦，并证明物理时间动力学的唯一性与正则性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法难以在无轨迹信息条件下学习高维概率密度的时间演化动力学，且无法处理旋转、循环等非梯度物理现象。

Method: 引入两参数流：先学习从基分布到每个边际分布的采样时间传输，再通过合成轨迹回归提取物理时间速度场，并利用条件流匹配技术避免最优传输耦合。

Result: 证明了物理时间动力学的唯一性，并继承了采样时间传输的正则性；方法可扩展至高维，支持非梯度动力学（如旋转、循环现象）。

Conclusion: 本文方法为无轨迹信息下的高维概率动力学学习提供了可扩展且理论完备的框架，尤其适用于涉及旋转等非梯度物理过程的场景。

Abstract: This work addresses the problem of learning the dynamics of high-dimensional probability densities over time using unlabeled samples, without assuming access to trajectory information. We introduce two-parameter flows that learn only sampling-time transports from a base distribution to each marginal and then extract a physics-time velocity by regressing on coupled synthetic trajectories. We prove that the resulting physics-time dynamics are unique and inherit regularity from the sampling-time transports. Because we can build on standard, well-developed conditional flow matching techniques for learning the base-to-marginal transports, our approach scales to high dimensions and avoids per-step optimal-transport couplings, while allowing admissible non-gradient dynamics that can naturally explain rotational or circulating physics phenomena.

</details>


### [118] [Stateful Inference for Low-Latency Multi-Agent Tool Calling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26289)
*Victor Norgren*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种有状态推理架构，通过持久化KV缓存、基数前缀缓存和提示查找投机解码，将多智能体工具调用的每轮推理成本从O(n_t)降低到O(Δ_t)，在6轮工作流中每轮加速2.1倍，35轮工作流中中位数加速4.2倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM推理框架将每轮工具调用视为独立请求，重复处理整个对话历史（85-95%的提示内容不变），导致计算效率极低。

Method: 1) 持久化KV缓存：跨轮次存活，仅增量处理新token；2) 基数前缀缓存：扩展至多智能体间的交错流量；3) 提示查找投机解码器：加速结构化输出。

Result: 对比vLLM和SGLang，在6轮智能体工作流中每轮加速2.1倍，35轮工作流中位数加速4.2倍，端到端时间减半。

Conclusion: 优势来源于有状态重用和投机解码，而非传统缓存机制。

Abstract: Multi-agent tool calling is becoming the dominant interaction pattern for LLM-based systems, yet existing inference frameworks treat each tool call as an independent request, re-processing the entire conversation from scratch even though 85-95% of the prompt is unchanged from the previous turn.
  We present a stateful inference architecture that converts the $O(n_t)$ per-turn cost of conventional serving into an $O(Δ_t)$ delta-only cost: a persistent KV cache lives across turns and advances by ingesting only the new tokens, while a radix prefix cache extends this across interleaved multi-agent traffic and a prompt-lookup speculative decoder accelerates structured output. Against vLLM and SGLang on novel, fully-generated workloads, the reference implementation is $2.1\times$ faster per turn on a 6-turn agentic workflow and $4.2\times$ on the median turn of a 35-turn one, halving end-to-end wall time. The advantage comes from stateful reuse and speculation, not caching.

</details>


### [119] [Curriculum Learning for Safety Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26315)
*Sandeep Kumar,Virginia Smith,Chhavi Yadav*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Staged-Competence课程学习框架，通过按难度组织偏好数据、能力感知采样和渐进更新参考模型，显著提升DPO安全对齐的鲁棒性和OOD泛化能力，平均降低16%有害响应率和20%越狱成功率，同时保持通用能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DPO方法在安全对齐中脆弱且OOD泛化能力差，亟需提升其鲁棒性。

Method: 提出Staged-Competence课程学习框架，包含三个核心：按难度组织偏好数据、基于模型当前能力的采样策略、训练过程中渐进更新参考模型。

Result: 跨三个模型家族平均：OOD有害响应率降低16%，越狱攻击成功率降低20%，仅用75%训练数据即可达到基线安全水平，且几乎不增加过度拒绝。

Conclusion: 课程学习能有效提升DPO安全对齐的鲁棒性，该框架与策略优化损失无关，可扩展到其他DPO变体和对齐领域。

Abstract: Direct Preference Optimisation (DPO) is widely used for safety alignment in large language models. However, prior work shows it is brittle and exhibits poor out-of-distribution (OOD) generalisation. In this paper, we investigate whether Curriculum Learning can improve the robustness of DPO-based safety alignment. We propose Staged-Competence, a curriculum-based framework that organises preference data by difficulty, employs competence-based sampling, and progressively updates the reference model during training. Averaged across three model families, Staged-Competence reduces OOD harmful response rates by 16% and jailbreak attack success rates by 20%, while preserving general capabilities with near-zero over-refusal. We further show that Staged-Competence (1) matches baseline safety with only 75% of the training data and (2) yields better separation between safe and unsafe responses. Staged-Competence is agnostic to the policy optimisation loss and can extend to other DPO variants and alignment domains. Our code and data are available at https://github.com/Sandeep5500/curriculum-learning-for-safety.

</details>


### [120] [Semigroup Consistency as a Diagnostic for Learned Physics Simulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26324)
*Lennon J. Shikhman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出归一化半群误差（Normalized Semigroup Error）作为评估学习型物理模拟器长期推演能力的诊断指标，通过比较直接推演与组合推演的一致性来检测时间组合中的失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统单步或短时预测误差无法捕捉长时间推演中的组合失效问题，需要一种能够评估时间组合一致性的指标。

Method: 利用自治、状态完备系统的解映满足半群律（semigroup law）这一性质，提出归一化半群误差指标，通过比较直接推演$t+s$步与先推演$s$步再推演$t$步的预测差异来诊断模型。

Result: 在一维热方程和Burgers方程上，以ConvNet和FNO为基线，半群误差与推演退化呈正相关，轨迹级Spearman相关系数$\rho=0.635$，95%置信区间$[0.621, 0.649]$。

Conclusion: 半群误差主要适合作为评估诊断工具，而非通用的训练目标；半群正则化的效果不一。

Abstract: Learned physics simulators are often evaluated by one-step or short-horizon prediction error, but these metrics can miss failures in temporal composition and long-horizon rollout. For autonomous, state-complete systems, exact solution maps satisfy a semigroup law: direct evolution over $s+t$ should agree with evolution over $s$ followed by $t$. We propose normalized semigroup error as a post hoc, model-agnostic diagnostic comparing these direct and composed learned predictions. On one-dimensional heat and Burgers dynamics with time-conditioned ConvNet and FNO baselines, semigroup error is positively associated with rollout degradation, with trajectory-level Spearman correlation $ρ= 0.635$ and $95%$ CI $[0.621, 0.649]$. Semigroup regularization has mixed effects, supporting semigroup consistency primarily as an evaluation diagnostic rather than a universally beneficial training objective.

</details>


### [121] [QAM-W: Joint 2D Codebook Quantization for LLM Weights via Hadamard Rotation and Activation-Aware Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26339)
*Preetam Sharma,Kacper Dobek*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: QAM-W是一种新的权重量化编解码器，通过L2归一化、块哈达玛旋转、2D坐标配对和基于单位圆高斯分布的劳埃德-麦克斯码本量化，在约5.5 bpw位宽下，跨5个LLM（1.1B-13B参数）的WikiText-2困惑度与BF16偏差在±0.4%以内，匹配SmoothQuant W8A8质量但减少32%权重位数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标量后训练量化器丢弃了权重行内的成对坐标结构，QAM-W旨在通过恢复这种结构来提升量化效率。

Method: 每行进行L2归一化→块哈达玛旋转→配对为2D坐标→使用基于单位圆高斯分布训练的劳埃德-麦克斯码本进行量化，并结合激活感知的逐通道缩放。

Result: 在5个LLM（4个系列，1.1B-13B参数）和8个量化配置中，约5.5 bpw的激活感知变体在每模型上与BF16的困惑度偏差在±0.4%内；2D联合编码在等位率下比极坐标编码ΔPPL提升2-15个百分点；成对KL与BF16的ΔPPL%在37个（方法,模型）上的斯皮尔曼ρ=0.99。

Conclusion: QAM-W在5-6 bpw波段实现了高质量保持的量化，3.5 bpw变体在量化容忍架构上有竞争力；在严格4 bpw下QTIP更优。

Abstract: Scalar post-training quantizers discard pairwise coordinate structure within weight rows. We introduce QAM-W (Quadrature Amplitude Modulation for Weights), a codec that recovers this structure: each row is L2-normalized, block-Hadamard rotated, paired into 2D coordinates, and quantized against a single Lloyd-Max codebook trained on the unit circular Gaussian, with activation-aware per-channel scaling. In a cross-model study spanning five LLMs from four families (1.1B--13B parameters) and eight quantized configurations, the activation-aware variant at $\approx 5.5$ bpw stays within $\pm 0.4\%$ of BF16 WikiText-2 perplexity on every model, matching the SmoothQuant W8A8 quality envelope at $32\%$ fewer weight bits. Joint 2D coding outperforms polar (amplitude $\times$ phase) coding by 2--15~pp $Δ$PPL at equal bitrate, and paired KL against BF16 tracks $Δ$PPL\% at Spearman $ρ= 0.99$ across 37 (method, model) rows, consistent with a monotone composite bound from codec distortion to KL divergence. A 3.5~bpw variant is competitive on quantization-tolerant architectures. At strict 4~bpw, the rotated-codebook frontier method QTIP outperforms QAM-W; the contribution is the quality-preserving 5--6~bpw band.

</details>


### [122] [When Correct Demonstrations Hurt: Rethinking the Role of Exemplars in In-Context Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26350)
*Chenghao Qiu,Chunli Peng,Yufeng Yang,Kuan-Hao Huang,Yi Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现一个反直觉现象：在上下文学习（ICL）中，正确的示范并不一定有用，甚至可能降低准确率。作者提出“任务保持扰动”框架，发现这种扰动会改变模型用于上下文推理的证据分布，导致“上下文证据偏移”，从而损害ICL性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观点认为ICL中提供正确的输入-输出示范有助于推理，但作者观察到存在“正确性-效用差距”——正确示范有时反而降低性能，亟需解释这一矛盾。

Method: 提出任务保持扰动（task-preserving perturbations）：只改变示范输入，但输出仍保持任务映射的正确性。分为标签更新扰动（任务语义改变，重新计算目标）和标签保持扰动（原目标仍有效）。通过该框架形式化“上下文证据偏移”这一失效模式。

Result: 在情感分类、逻辑推理和数学应用题上，任务保持扰动的示范会显著降低ICL性能，尤其对更小的模型、更困难的任务和更高的扰动比例影响更大。

Conclusion: ICL的鲁棒性不仅需要评估示范是否正确，还需要评估它们如何影响模型的上下文推理过程。

Abstract: In-context learning (ICL) is often motivated by the intuition that demonstrations help because they provide correct input-output examples. However, we reveal a counterintuitive phenomenon: correctness does not guarantee exemplar utility, and some correct demonstrations can even reduce ICL accuracy. To study this correctness-utility gap, we introduce task-preserving perturbations, where only the exemplar input is changed, while the example remains a correct instance of the same task. Concretely, each perturbed exemplar is assigned the target induced by the task mapping. This framework covers both label-updating perturbations, where task-relevant semantics change and targets are recomputed, and stricter target-preserving perturbations, where the original target remains valid. We formalize the resulting failure mode as contextual evidence shift: task-preserving perturbations can change the effective mixture of evidence used by the model for contextual inference, thereby separating exemplar correctness from exemplar utility. Across sentiment classification, logical reasoning, and math word problems, we find that task-preserving perturbed demonstrations can substantially degrade ICL performance, especially for smaller models, harder tasks, and higher perturbation ratios. Our results show that robust ICL requires evaluating not only whether demonstrations are correct, but also how they influence contextual inference. Code is available at https://github.com/Chenghao-Qiu/Task-Preserving-ICL.

</details>


### [123] [Balancing Plasticity and Stability with Fast and Slow Successor Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26357)
*Raymond Chua,Doina Precup,Blake Richards*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究深度强化学习在渐进式环境变化中的适应性，发现稳定性（突触巩固）优于可塑性（参数重置），且多时间尺度巩固后继特征（SFs）方法表现最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实环境通常通过逐渐漂移演变，而非突变，但现有研究多关注突变式非平稳性。这种区别对强化学习中的"稳定-可塑性困境"有重要影响，因此需要系统研究渐进式非平稳性下的适应性机制。

Method: 修改3D Miniworld和MuJoCo环境引入自然主义渐进式非平稳性，比较稳定性方法（突触巩固）与可塑性方法（参数重置）的性能；进一步探索将后继特征（SFs）作为巩固目标的优势，并引入多时间尺度巩固策略。

Result: 稳定性方法（突触巩固）优于可塑性方法（参数重置）；将突触巩固应用于SFs比应用于Q值表现更好；多时间尺度巩固SFs能捕捉环境变化的互补方面，取得最优性能。

Conclusion: 在渐进式变化中，稳定性比可塑性更关键；多时间尺度巩固预测表征是处理持续环境漂移的有效方法。

Abstract: A hallmark of intelligence is the ability to adapt in non-stationary environments, yet deep Reinforcement Learning (RL) agents often struggle in such settings. Prior studies introduce non-stationarity through abrupt shifts in features or dynamics, whereas real-world environments often evolve gradually through continual drift. This distinction has important implications for the "stability-plasticity dilemma" in RL, as abrupt task changes may demand more plasticity than naturalistic settings. To address this, we modify existing 3D Miniworld and MuJoCo environments to incorporate naturalistic, continual non-stationarity, and use them to examine how stability and adaptation affect performance under continuous environmental change. We find that methods favoring stability, such as synaptic consolidation, outperform approaches focused on plasticity, such as parameters resetting. Motivated by this result, and prior evidence that Successor Features (SFs) reduce interference, we investigate whether SFs are better consolidation targets than Q-values. Across both environments, applying neuro-inspired synaptic consolidation to SFs yields superior performance on continually changing settings. Moreover, consolidation is most effective when SFs are stabilized across multiple timescales, which capture complementary aspects of gradual environmental change. Together, these results suggest that stability is more critical in continual learning when changes are gradual, and that multi-timescale consolidation of predictive representations is an effective approach.

</details>


### [124] [Online Learning on Hidden-Convex Losses via Algorithmic Equivalence: Optimal Regret, Geometric Barrier, and Bandit Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26373)
*Anas Barakat,Andreas Kontogiannis,Vasilis Pollatos,Ioannis Panageas,Antonios Varvitsiotis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明，在隐藏凸损失假设下，在线梯度下降（OGD）可以实现最优的$\mathcal{O}(\sqrt{T})$遗憾界，并给出了 Hessian 相容性这一必要且充分的几何条件，同时将结果扩展到单点赌博反馈设置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前工作（Ghai, Lu & Hazan, 2022）证明在隐藏凸损失下 OGD 可模拟在线镜像下降（OMD）并获得$\mathcal{O}(T^{2/3})$遗憾，但能否达到最优的$\Theta(\sqrt{T})$遗憾是该领域的公开问题。

Method: 通过更精确的离散时间算法等价性论证，证明 OGD 在相同假设下达到$\mathcal{O}(\sqrt{T})$遗憾；将对角雅可比充分条件替换为必要的 Hessian 相容性条件；并通过构造反例证明该条件的必要性；最后扩展到单点赌博反馈。

Result: ① OGD 在隐藏凸损失下达到$\mathcal{O}(\sqrt{T})$最优遗憾界；② 提出 Hessian 相容性条件，放宽了可允许的重参数化类别；③ 当条件不满足时，OGD 遭受$\Omega(T)$遗憾；④ 在单点赌博反馈下，带球形平滑的 OGD 达到$\mathcal{O}(T^{3/4})$期望遗憾。

Conclusion: 该工作解决了隐藏凸学习中的核心开放问题，建立了与在线凸优化相同的最优速率，并阐明了算法等价所需的精确几何条件，显著扩展了理论适用范围。

Abstract: We study adversarial online learning with hidden-convex losses, i.e., nonconvex losses that become convex after a nonlinear reparameterization. Ghai, Lu and Hazan (2022) proved that, under geometric and smoothness assumptions, online gradient descent (OGD) on such nonconvex losses approximately simulates online mirror descent (OMD) on the underlying convex losses with a suitable regularizer, yielding $\mathcal{O}(T^{2/3})$ regret. They left open whether the optimal $Θ(\sqrt{T})$ regret from online convex optimization can be recovered in this hidden-convex setting. We answer this question affirmatively. More specifically, via a sharper discrete-time algorithmic equivalence argument, we prove that OGD achieves $\mathcal{O}(\sqrt{T})$ regret under the same assumptions, matching the optimal worst-case rate for adversarial online convex optimization. We also address another open question of Ghai, Lu and Hazan (2022) by clarifying the geometry required for this algorithmic equivalence. We replace the diagonal-Jacobian sufficient condition with a necessary-and-sufficient Hessian compatibility condition, thereby expanding the class of admissible reparameterizations. We complement our tight regret bound with a lower bound showing that the Hessian compatibility assumption is essential for OGD; when it fails, we construct a smooth reparameterization and an adversarial sequence of hidden-convex losses for which OGD suffers $Ω(T)$ regret. Finally, we extend our analysis to one-point bandit feedback and prove a $\mathcal{O}(T^{3/4})$ expected regret bound for bandit OGD with spherical smoothing, matching its classical rate on convex losses.

</details>


### [125] [Function-Valued Causal Influence in Nonlinear Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26408)
*Valentina V. Kuskova,Dmitry Zaytsev,Michael Coppedge*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文指出，在时间序列因果发现中使用非线性机器学习模型时，传统的标量边评分会严重信息损失，并提出了基于函数值的因果影响分析框架，通过个体条件期望（ICE）直接从训练模型中估计因果响应函数，揭示了传统方法无法发现的复杂因果结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列因果发现方法几乎总是用标量边评分来总结非线性模型学到的因果关系，但这种做法掩盖了非线性自回归模型实际学到的状态依赖函数，其效应随状态、幅度和上下文变化。标量因果评分构成了严重的信息瓶颈，混淆了状态间变异与状态内残差噪声。

Method: 以神经加性向量自回归模型为代表架构，提出了基于个体条件期望（ICE）的实用框架，直接从训练好的模型中估计因果响应函数。通过受控合成实验和民主发展案例研究进行验证。

Result: 实验表明，具有不可区分标量分数的边可以表现出截然不同的函数行为，包括单调、阈值、饱和和符号翻转效应。案例研究进一步揭示，基于函数值的分析能发现传统评分方法系统性遗漏的状态依赖和非对称因果结构。

Conclusion: 标量因果评分在实践中会造成严重的信息瓶颈，函数值的因果分析能够揭示更丰富、更准确的因果结构信息，对于理解复杂动态系统中的因果关系至关重要。

Abstract: Causal discovery in time series is increasingly performed using nonlinear machine-learning models, yet the resulting causal relationships are almost always summarized by scalar edge scores. We argue that this practice obscures the true object learned by nonlinear autoregressive models: a state-dependent function whose effect varies across regimes, magnitudes, and contexts. We formalize function-valued causal influence for additive, contribution-decomposable architectures and show that scalar causal scores constitute a severe information bottleneck, conflating between-state variation with within-state residual noise. Using Neural Additive Vector Autoregression as a representative architecture, we introduce a practical framework based on Individual Conditional Expectation for estimating causal response functions directly from trained models. Through controlled synthetic experiments, we demonstrate that edges with indistinguishable scalar scores can exhibit qualitatively different functional behaviors, including monotonic, thresholded, saturating, and sign-changing effects. An applied case study on democratic development further shows that function-valued analysis reveals regime-specific and asymmetric causal structure systematically missed by score-centric approaches.

</details>


### [126] [FM-fMRI: Event Conditioned Flow Matching for Rest-to-Task fMRI Time-Series Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26423)
*Peiyu Duan,Jiyao Wang,Nicha C. Dvornek,Junlin Yang,Ziqi Gao,Lawrence H. Staib,James S. Duncan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FM-fMRI，一种基于流匹配的事件条件生成模型，可以从静息态fMRI和任务事件信息生成任务态fMRI时间序列。在HCP和BioPoint自闭症队列上，该方法在谱一致性、连接性匹配和分布对齐方面优于扩散模型、GAN和VAE基线，并通过合成数据提升了自闭症分类性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 任务态fMRI能直接反映任务诱发的神经活动，但获取成本高、难以大规模采集。利用广泛可得的静息态fMRI合成任务态fMRI数据，可以缓解数据稀缺问题。

Method: 提出FM-fMRI，一个事件条件流匹配模型，学习连续时间条件向量场，从静息态fMRI和任务事件信息生成任务ROI时间序列。采用快速ODE采样，支持灵活的事件调度条件。评估时不局限于逐点重建，而是从时间/谱结构、连接组一致性、分布对齐等多个维度衡量。

Result: 在HCP和BioPoint自闭症队列上，FM-fMRI在谱一致性、连接性匹配和分布级对齐上均优于扩散模型、GAN和VAE基线。通过合成任务fMRI数据，提升了自闭症分类的准确率。

Conclusion: FM-fMRI能有效从静息态fMRI合成高质量任务态fMRI信号，在数据有限的临床场景中具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: Task-based fMRI provides a direct readout of task-evoked neural dynamics, but it is expensive and difficult to acquire at scale, motivating rest-to-task synthesis from widely available resting-state fMRI (rsfMRI). We propose FM-fMRI, an event-conditioned flow-matching model that learns a continuous-time conditional vector field to generate task ROI time series from a subject's rsfMRI and the task event information. The formulation enables fast ODE-based sampling and flexible conditioning over heterogeneous event schedules. Rather than optimizing for pointwise reconstruction, we evaluated generated signals using complementary criteria that probe temporal and spectral structure, subject and group-level connectome consistency, and distributional alignment. On the public Human Connectome Project and internal BioPoint autism cohort, FM-fMRI achieves the strongest spectral and connectivity agreement and improved distribution-level matching over conditional diffusion, generative adversarial networks (GANs), and variational autoencoders (VAEs) baselines. Furthermore, we augment the BioPoint cohort by synthesizing task-fMRI ROI time series with our method, improving downstream autism classification and demonstrating practical utility in data-limited clinical settings. The code will be available on GitHub.

</details>


### [127] [Aperiodic and Low-Frequency Spectral Bias in Reconstruction based EEG Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26434)
*Aditya Kommineni,Emily Zhou,Kleanthis Avramidis,Simon Bock Segaard,Jeppe Roden Münster,Andreas Peter Juhl Hansen,Takfarinas Medani,Tiantian Feng,Richard Leahy,Shrikanth Narayanan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了基于重构的EEG基础模型在低资源场景下表现不佳的机制原因：模型偏向学习高功率的非周期性成分而忽略高频振荡成分，且编码更多受试者身份信息而非任务相关信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究EEG基础模型在低资源场景下性能不如小规模监督模型的原因，为改进EEG基础模型提供理论指导。

Method: 使用合成EEG数据进行受控实验分析模型嵌入的偏向性，并在真实BCI数据集上进行线性探针评估，验证模型对非周期成分和高频振荡成分的表征差异。

Result: EEG基础模型嵌入偏向捕获非周期成分而低估高频振荡成分；在真实数据上，嵌入编码的受试者身份信息比任务相关信息更强。

Conclusion: 重构损失导致EEG基础模型存在表征偏差，未来需引入显式针对高频振荡结构的辅助损失函数以提升模型能力。

Abstract: EEG foundation models, pre-trained on large-scale unlabelled EEG data, have emerged as a promising direction towards learning generalizable EEG representations. Despite showing positive results in data-rich regimes, they often fail to outperform significantly smaller supervised models in low-resource settings compared to fully supervised models. We provide a mechanistic account of this shortcoming, attributing it to a fundamental mismatch between reconstruction-based pretext tasks and the idiosyncratic spectral structure of EEG signals, which decompose into distinct high-power aperiodic and low-power oscillatory components. Using controlled, synthetically-generated EEG inputs, we demonstrate that EEG foundation model embeddings are biased to capture the aperiodic components of the EEG signal while under-representing oscillatory components, particularly at higher frequencies. Additionally, linear probe evaluations on real-world BCI datasets further reveal that embeddings encode subject identity more strongly than task-relevant information, thereby reinforcing the low-frequency and aperiodic component bias in foundation model embeddings trained primarily on reconstruction based objectives. Together, these findings elucidate a failure mode in reconstruction based EEG foundation models and motivate future work to incorporate auxiliary losses explicitly targeting high-frequency oscillatory structure as a path toward more capable and generalizable EEG representations.

</details>


### [128] [DDGAD: Trajectory Dynamics for Diffusion-Based Graph Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26446)
*Yuxin Yang,Limei Hu,Feng Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DDGAD是一种基于扩散过程的图异常检测框架，利用轨迹动力学区分正常节点和异常节点，通过可靠性感知的共识机制和三种互补异常信号有效缓解了GCN中存在的污染传播问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于GCN的图异常检测方法存在严重的污染传播问题——异常节点通过消息传递污染邻居节点表征，导致检测性能下降。作者旨在解决这一根本性缺陷。

Method: 提出DDGAD框架，核心包括：(1) 扩散正则化与可靠性感知邻居共识机制；(2) 分布式可靠性感知共识精炼机制；(3) 三种互补异常信号：邻居不一致性、可靠性权重和动力学冲突能量；(4) 对正常节点在耦合动力学下的稳定性进行了初步理论分析。

Result: 在五个真实数据集上的大量实验验证了该框架的有效性。

Conclusion: 正常节点在扩散正则化和可靠性感知共识的耦合作用下表现出稳定一致的表示轨迹，而异常节点因全局流形先验与局部污染消息传递之间的方向不一致，表现出不稳定和冲突的动力学特性，从而可被有效检测。

Abstract: Graph anomaly detection (GAD) aims to identify nodes or substructures whose behavior or attributes deviate significantly from the overall pattern in graph-structured data, with critical applications in financial risk control, social network analysis, and cybersecurity. However, existing GCN-based methods suffer from the fundamental problem of contamination propagation, where anomalous nodes pollute the representations of their neighbors through message passing, leading to degraded detection performance. In this paper, we propose DDGAD, a novel diffusion-based graph anomaly detection framework that leverages trajectory dynamics to distinguish normal and anomalous nodes. Our key insight is that normal nodes exhibit consistent and stable representation trajectories under the coupled effects of diffusion regularization and reliability-aware neighborhood consensus, while anomalous nodes exhibit unstable and conflicting dynamics due to the directional disagreement between the global manifold prior and locally contaminated message passing. To mitigate contamination propagation, we introduce a distributed reliability-aware consensus refinement mechanism and define three complementary anomaly signals: neighbor inconsistency, reliability weight, and dynamical conflict energy. We further provide a preliminary theoretical analysis on normal node stability under the coupled dynamics. These signals collectively characterize anomalous behaviors from the perspectives of local inconsistency, consensus reliability, and dynamical instability. Extensive experiments on five real-world datasets demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed framework.

</details>


### [129] [MuCon: Clipped Muon Updates for LLM Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26459)
*Albert Yi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MuCon优化器对Muon矩阵进行奇异值裁剪（将超过阈值τ的奇异值压缩到τ），替代Muon的极分解方法。本文分析了裁剪操作的数学性质、投影解释，并探讨了无需完整SVD的近似计算方案及其数值稳定性挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Muon-style优化器使用矩阵值动量或预条件更新，但极分解将非零奇异值全部映射为1，可能丢失有用信息。MuCon通过裁剪奇异值（仅限制过大值，保留小值）来缓解这一问题，同时保持优化轨迹的稳定性。

Method: 提出MuCon优化器原语，使用MClip_τ算子对Muon矩阵B的奇异值进行阈值裁剪（min(σ_i, τ)）。给出了两种精确恒等式：极/绝对值公式和标量根公式，并引入有理牛顿滤波器计算裁剪后的半正定因子。

Result: 发现两种公式的共同数值障碍：当奇异值接近阈值τ时，符号决策和有理求解会变得病态。因此，矩阵函数方法需配合稳定的极分解/平方根本原或裁剪边界附近的显式正则化才能实用。

Conclusion: MuCon裁剪操作在理论上可投影到谱范数球上，但高效稳定的近似实现需要解决阈值附近的数值不稳定性问题，完全避免密集SVD仍面临挑战。

Abstract: Muon-style optimizers take a matrix-valued momentum or preconditioned update $B = U \operatorname{diag}(σ_1,\ldots,σ_r) V^\top$ and replace it with its canonical partial polar factor $\operatorname{Pol}(B) = U V^\top$. This maps every nonzero singular value to one. MuCon is the clipped-Muon variant studied here: it applies singular-value clipping to the same Muon matrix, $D^{\mathrm{MuCon}}\_τ(B) = \operatorname{MClip}\_τ(B) = U \operatorname{diag}\bigl(\min\{σ\_i,τ\}\bigr) V^\top, \qquad τ> 0$. Thus, $\operatorname{MClip}\_τ$ denotes the mathematical clipping operator, while MuCon denotes the optimizer primitive that substitutes this clipped direction for Muon's polar direction. The Muon/MuCon scaling parameterization used in this work is called $\text{SpectralP}$: it is the hidden-matrix scaling recipe under which polar Muon or clipped MuCon directions are applied. The map $\operatorname{MClip}\_τ$ is the Frobenius projection onto the spectral-norm ball $\{X : \|X\|_2 \le τ\}$: it leaves singular values at or below $τ$ unchanged and modifies only the violating singular directions. This paper asks when the MuCon clipping step can be approximated without a full dense SVD. We record two exact identities, a polar/absolute-value formula and a scalar-root formulation leading to a rational Newton filter for the clipped positive-semidefinite factor, and identify the numerical obstruction common to both: singular values near the threshold make sign decisions and rational solves ill-conditioned. Matrix-function methods are therefore useful only when paired with stable polar/square-root primitives or explicit regularization near the clipping boundary.

</details>


### [130] [Diffuse to Detect: Generative Diffusion Models for Unsupervised IC Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26468)
*Yuxuan Yin,Chen He,Todd Jacobs,Jialei He,Boxun Xu,Robert Jin,Peng Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出首个结合扩散Transformer的无监督异常检测框架，通过自编码器压缩测试数据、结构化token序列和位置嵌入，利用中程扩散步长的噪声预测误差进行异常评分，在16nm IC测试数据上达到最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有潜在缺陷筛选面临极低故障率、高维测试数据和缺乏标注异常样本的挑战，亟需无需标注数据或手工特征工程的自动化检测方法。

Method: (1) 自编码器压缩原始测试数据；(2) 重组为结构化token序列，加入正弦位置编码和晶圆级位置嵌入；(3) 利用扩散Transformer在中程扩散步长上计算噪声预测误差作为异常分数。

Result: 在工业16nm IC测试数据上，在极端类别不平衡条件下取得最先进性能，并通过潜在空间重建残差实现可解释的故障定位。

Conclusion: 该无监督框架无需标注缺陷或手工特征，即可实现快速晶圆级筛选，兼具高性能与可解释性。

Abstract: Latent defect screening is challenged by extremely low failure rates, high-dimensional test data, and absence of labeled anomalies. We propose the first unsupervised anomaly detection framework incorporating a Diffusion Transformer. Raw test measurements are first compressed by an autoencoder, then reshaped into a structured token sequence enriched with sinusoidal and per-device wafer-position embeddings. Anomaly scores are derived from the noise-prediction error over mid-range diffusion timesteps, enabling fast wafer-scale screening without any labeled defects or manual feature engineering. Our approach achieves state-of-the-art performance on industrial 16nm IC test data under extreme class imbalance, offering interpretable failure localization through latent-space reconstruction residuals.

</details>


### [131] [Extra-Merge: Tracing the Rank-1 Subspace of Model Merging in Language Model Pre-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26484)
*Wenjie Zhou,Bohan Wang,Hongtao Zhang,Chenxi Jia,Wei Chen,Xueqi Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现大模型训练后期存在"秩-1子空间"现象：连续的模型检查点合并后收敛到近似一维线性流形。基于此提出Extra-Merge方法，通过沿该子空间外推来降低损失，无需额外梯度更新，在GPT-2、LLaMA等模型上表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型合并（Model Merging）虽已成为增强大语言模型的轻量级范式，但其底层机制尚未被充分理解。作者旨在揭示合并操作背后的几何结构，并据此提出更优的合并策略。

Method: 首先分析训练后期轨迹，发现秩-1子空间现象（Raw优化步长剧烈震荡，但合并后的检查点坍缩到近似一维线性流形）。通过"河谷"（river-valley）景观分析从理论上解释：平均操作作为几何低通滤波器，抑制高曲率噪声，揭示最优下降方向。基于此提出Extra-Merge，一种无需训练的策略，沿该子空间外推以最小化损失。

Result: 在GPT-2和LLaMA系列（124M到2B参数）上，Extra-Merge持续优于标准合并基线。在Pythia-12B下游任务上获得一致的零样本准确率提升，并能有效泛化到Muon优化器。

Conclusion: 模型合并背后的几何结构可以被解释和利用，通过沿秩-1子空间外推可以在不增加训练成本的情况下提升模型性能，为大模型合并提供了理论基础和实用方法。

Abstract: Model merging has emerged as a lightweight paradigm for enhancing Large Language Models (LLMs), yet its underlying mechanisms remain poorly understood. In this work, we analyze late-stage pre-training trajectories and uncover a \textbf{Rank-1 Subspace} phenomenon: while raw optimization steps oscillate violently, consecutive \emph{merged} checkpoints collapse onto a stable, approximately one-dimensional linear manifold. We theoretically ground this observation in a \emph{river-valley} landscape analysis: averaging acts as a geometric low-pass filter that dampens high-curvature noise to reveal the optimal descent direction. Capitalizing on this insight, we propose \textbf{Extra-Merge}, a training-free strategy that extrapolates along this subspace to minimize loss without additional gradient updates. Extensive experiments across GPT-2 and LLaMA families (124M to 2B) demonstrate that Extra-Merge consistently outperforms standard merging baselines. Notably, it yields consistent zero-shot accuracy gains on Pythia-12B downstream tasks and generalizes effectively to the Muon optimizer \citep{jordan2024muon}.

</details>


### [132] [The Stability of Singular Distribution: A Spectral Perspective on the Two-Phase Dynamics of Language Model Pre-training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26489)
*Hongtao Zhang,Wenjie Zhou,Chenxi Jia,Wei Chen,Xueqi Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文发现大语言模型预训练中存在谱现象SoSD（奇异分布稳定性），即迹归一化奇异值谱在参数矩阵仍在演化时早期就趋于稳定，且与慢速下降阶段同步。通过理论分析证明权重范数增长会触发SoSD阈值，之后损失下降速率受奇异分布变化的理论约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示大语言模型预训练中快速损失下降后进入缓慢改进阶段的底层谱机制，为理解训练动态提供新的理论视角。

Method: 通过实验观察GPT-2、LLaMA等不同架构下SoSD现象，结合简化Transformer的理论分析证明权重范数增长与SoSD阈值的关系，并解释WSD、Muon等策略通过调节SoSD尺度影响训练效率。

Result: SoSD现象在多种架构、调度策略、权重衰减和优化器中广泛存在；理论证明权重范数增长导致SoSD阈值，之后损失下降速率受奇异分布变化理论约束。

Conclusion: SoSD为大语言模型高效预训练提供了谱视角的理论解释，WSD和Muon等策略的有效性可归结为对SoSD尺度的调节。

Abstract: Large language model pre-training typically exhibits a two-phase trajectory: a fast initial loss drop followed by a prolonged slow improvement. We identify an underlying spectral phenomenon, Stability of Singular Distribution (SoSD), where the trace-normalized singular value spectrum stabilizes early, even as parameter matrices continue to evolve. We demonstrate that synchronization between SoSD and the slow-descent regime is widely observed across diverse architectures (GPT-2, LLaMA) and settings, including various schedules (Step-wise, WSD, Cosine Decay), weight decays, and optimizers (AdamW, Muon). By analyzing a simplified Transformer, we prove that growing weight norms inevitably precipitate an early SoSD threshold, after which the rate of loss decrease becomes theoretically bounded by the variation in the singular distribution. We further interpret strategies like WSD and Muon through their ability to modulate the SoSD scale, offering a spectral lens for understanding efficient pre-training dynamics.

</details>


### [133] [Beyond Pairwise Preferences: Listwise Reward-Aware Alignment for Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26491)
*Austin Wang,Jiaqi Han,Stefano Ermon,Yisong Yue*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Diffusion LAIR，一种基于奖励感知的列表级偏好优化方法，用于扩散模型的对齐。它利用多张候选图像的连续奖励分数，通过优势加权回归和二次惩罚项进行优化，在隐式奖励空间中存在有界闭式最优解，并在多个基准上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有偏好优化方法将监督信号简化为二值成对比较，忽略了训练数据中天然存在的多候选图像以及连续奖励分数所能提供的更丰富信息。

Method: 将一组候选图像的奖励分数转化为中心化优势权重，优化基于隐式奖励（当前模型相较于固定参考模型的去噪损失改进）的优势加权回归目标，并加入二次惩罚项以正则化隐式奖励的幅度。该目标同时在所有候选上优化，且在隐式奖励空间中有有界闭式最优解。

Result: 在SD1.5和SDXL上，Diffusion LAIR在文本到图像生成、组合生成和图像编辑基准上均优于强基线方法。

Conclusion: 列表级偏好优化结合连续奖励信号能更有效地对齐扩散模型，且正则化强度控制偏好更新的幅度，为偏好优化提供了理论上有保障的优化框架。

Abstract: Preference optimization has emerged as an efficient alternative to online reinforcement learning from human feedback (RLHF) for aligning text-to-image diffusion models. However, existing methods largely reduce supervision to binary pairwise comparisons. This pairwise reduction is limiting when training data naturally contains multiple candidate images for the same prompt, and when continuous reward scores can provide richer information than a single winner-loser label. To address these limitations, we propose Diffusion LAIR, a reward-aware listwise preference optimization method for diffusion models. For each prompt, LAIR converts reward scores across a group of candidate images into centered advantage weights, then optimizes an advantage-weighted regression objective on the implicit reward, defined as the denoising-loss improvement of the current model over a fixed reference model, with a quadratic penalty that regularizes the magnitude of the implicit reward. The resulting objective uses all candidates simultaneously rather than selecting pairs, and remains conservative by explicitly controlling the magnitude of the implicit reward. The LAIR objective admits a bounded closed-form optimum in implicit-reward space, clarifying how the regularization strength controls the magnitude of the preference update. Experiments show that Diffusion LAIR outperforms strong preference optimization baselines on SD1.5 and SDXL across text-to-image generation, compositional generation, and image editing benchmarks.

</details>


### [134] [Dense2MoE: Pushing the Pareto Frontier of On-Device LLMs via Unified Pruning and Upcycling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26496)
*Fengfa Li,Hongjin Ji,Yifeng Ding,Lei Ren,Chen Wei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Dense2MoE框架，通过层融合上循环技术，将稠密语言模型高效转化为适用于边缘设备的MoE模型，平衡了推理效率与模型精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MoE模型训练成本高昂，而将稠密模型上循环为MoE存在参数冗余问题，标准层剪枝又会损害精度。需要一种既能减少冗余又能保持性能的高效转换方法。

Method: 提出Layer Fusion UpCycling（LF-UC）方法，基于硬件Roofline理论，剪枝冗余层的带宽密集型注意力模块，同时将其MLP重新利用为MoE专家，并通过选择性令牌路由限制活跃参数。

Result: 在边缘设备推理延迟与模型精度的帕累托前沿上，Dense2MoE显著优于稠密基线、最先进的压缩方法和标准上循环方法。

Conclusion: Dense2MoE能以适度的持续预训练预算，高效地将公开的稠密大语言模型转换为适用于边缘设备的MoE模型，有效解决了推理内存墙问题。

Abstract: The Mixture of Experts MoE architecture is highly promising for resource constrained on device deployments yet training these models from scratch incurs prohibitive costs Current methods attempt to alleviate this by upcycling dense models into MoEs however they often introduce parameter redundancy that degrades inference efficiency Alternatively standard layer pruning mitigates redundancy but inevitably compromises model accuracy To resolve this dilemma we propose Dense2MoE a novel framework that unifies pruning and upcycling through Layer Fusion UpCycling LF UC Guided by hardware Roofline theory Dense2MoE systematically overcomes the inference memory wall by pruning bandwidth heavy attention modules from redundant layers while repurposing their Multi Layer Perceptrons MLPs into MoE experts This structural innovation preserves the models core capabilities and strictly limits active parameters via selective token routing With a modest continual pre training budget Dense2MoE efficiently converts publicly available dense LLMs into on device ready MoE models Extensive experiments demonstrate that Dense2MoE significantly advances the Pareto frontier for on device inference latency versus model accuracy outperforming dense baselines state of the art compression and standard upcycling methods

</details>


### [135] [Open-Weight LLM Fine-Tuning Defenses are Susceptible to Simple Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26526)
*Kevin Kuo,Chhavi Yadav,Virginia Smith*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现针对开源大语言模型的防御措施存在漏洞，仅通过abliteration和prefilling两种低成本攻击（无需梯度优化）即可在多个安全基准上将攻击成功率从低于10%提升至16%-96%。作者提出abliteration-resistant tuning (ART)方法，可将攻击成功率降低10%-20%。研究表明开源模型的安全攻击面比之前认为的更广。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的开源大语言模型安全防御主要假设有害行为需要通过微调学习，但预训练模型已编码了大量有害知识。作者希望探究是否无需微调，仅通过越狱（jailbreaking）方式就能绕开安全防护，并验证现有防御体系对低成本攻击的脆弱性。

Method: 1) 评估两种无需梯度优化的低成本攻击方法：abliteration和prefilling；2) 在BeaverTails、HarmBench、AdvBench三个有害性评估基准上进行实验；3) 提出ART（abliteration-resistant tuning）方法，将abliteration目标融入训练过程。

Result: 1) 现有安全防御下，这两种攻击将成功率从低于10%提升至16%-96%；2) ART方法能将abliteration、prefilling及其组合攻击的成功率降低10%-20%。

Conclusion: 开源大语言模型的安全攻击面比此前描述的更广，对安全防御的评估应纳入更多样化的攻击策略，而不应仅限于对抗性微调（adversarial fine-tuning）场景。

Abstract: Recent defenses for safeguarding open-weight large language models (LLMs) are intended to prevent adversarial usage. Underlying these defenses is an assumption that new harmful behavior is learned through fine-tuning rather than elicited by jailbreaking the model. Yet, pretrained LLMs already encode substantial harmful knowledge across many domains, which raises an important question: can an adversary jailbreak safeguarded models, to achieve harmful usage without fine-tuning at all? In this paper, we show that open-weight safeguards are susceptible to simpler strategies that, despite being well known, have not been systematically evaluated against these safeguards. Specifically, we evaluate two low-cost attacks--abliteration and prefilling--that do not rely on gradient-based optimization. Across three harmfulness evaluation benchmarks (BeaverTails, HarmBench, and AdvBench), these attacks increase attack success rates against safeguarded open-weight models from below 10\% to a range of 16%-96%. To mitigate this vulnerability, we introduce abliteration-resistant tuning (ART), which incorporates an abliteration-based objective into training. ART can be layered onto existing defenses and reduces the success rates of abliteration, prefilling, and their combination by 10%-20%. These findings indicate that the attack surface for open-weight models is broader than previously characterized, and that evaluations of safeguarding defenses should incorporate a more diverse set of attack strategies beyond adversarial fine-tuning.

</details>


### [136] [Recursive Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26535)
*Jiahe Huang,Sihan Xu,Sharvaree Vadgama,Rose Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出递归流匹配（RecFM）框架，通过自一致性约束实现高保真、少步长的复杂时空动力学预测，在保持精度的同时实现最高20倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成模型在物理系统求解中面临速度与保真度之间的根本性权衡，高精度计算成本高昂，亟需一种兼具速度与精度的解决方案。

Method: 提出Recursive Flow Matching（RecFM）框架，通过强制自一致性约束来对齐不同离散化尺度下的轨迹，从而减少离散化误差。

Result: 相比领先的扩散仿真器实现最高20倍加速；相比原始流匹配降低超过15%的均方误差；在一步/少步（2-4步）生成中达到与多步求解器相当的性能。

Conclusion: RecFM是首个在科学系统中实现高保真一步/少步动态生成的方法，为实时科学仿真提供了可扩展且高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Generative models have emerged as a powerful paradigm for solving physics systems and modeling complex spatiotemporal dynamics. However, achieving high physical accuracy without incurring high computational cost remains a fundamental challenge, as existing approaches face a critical speed-fidelity trade-off. In this work, we introduce Recursive Flow Matching (RecFM), a generative framework for forecasting complex spatiotemporal dynamics. RecFM enforces self-consistency to align trajectories across discretization scales, reducing discretization errors and improving performance across metrics for physics-based tasks. To our knowledge, this is the first method to achieve high-fidelity one- and few-step (2-4 step) dynamic generation for scientific systems with performance comparable to state-of-the-art multi-step solvers. Across challenging scientific benchmarks, RecFM achieves up to a 20$\times$ speedup over leading diffusion-based emulators while improving predictive accuracy. Furthermore, RecFM reduces mean squared error by over 15% compared to vanilla flow matching, offering a scalable and efficient solution for real-time scientific emulation.

</details>


### [137] [Auditing and Fixing Economic Validity in Tabular Foundation Models for Discrete Choice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26559)
*Yingshuo Wang,Xian Sun,Yanhang Li,Zhichao Fan,Zexin Zhuang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种两阶段适配器，将表格基础模型嵌入效用最大化框架，在保证经济一致性的同时提升预测准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格基础模型在预测任务中准确率高，但常违背经济逻辑（如提价反而增加需求、支付意愿为负），需要在不牺牲准确性的前提下保证经济合理性。

Method: 两阶段适配器：第一阶段估计符合经济理论的约束参数的标准选择模型；第二阶段冻结这些参数，训练一个校正项以融入基础模型的预测信息。

Result: 在两个交通数据集上，适配器相比标准logit模型准确率提升高达13个百分点，同时保持完全的经济一致性，优于原始基础模型和传统蒸馏方法。

Conclusion: 该方法能继承基础模型的准确性优势，同时保证价格-需求关系的单调性和可解析计算的权衡指标，实现经济理论与数据驱动模型的融合。

Abstract: Tabular foundation models achieve strong accuracy on choice prediction tasks, but their predictions often violate the economic logic those tasks require: raising a price sometimes increases predicted demand, and implied willingness-to-pay estimates are frequently negative or implausible. We propose a two-stage adapter that embeds foundation model predictions within a utility-maximization framework. In the first stage, we estimate a standard choice model whose parameters are constrained to obey economic theory. In the second stage, we freeze those parameters and train a correction term that incorporates the foundation model's predictions as additional information. The result is a model that inherits the foundation model's accuracy gains while guaranteeing monotonic price-demand relationships under policy perturbation and producing analytically computable trade-off measures. On two transportation datasets, the adapter recovers up to 13 percentage points of accuracy over a standard logit model while maintaining perfect economic consistency, something neither the raw foundation models nor conventional distillation achieve.

</details>


### [138] [Distribution-Aware Conformal Prediction: A Framework for generating efficient prediction intervals for time series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26569)
*Daniel Schweizer,Peter Kuhn,Jayant Sharma,Shivali Dubey,Malte von Ramin,Christoph Brockt-Haßauer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出分布感知的共形预测（DCP）框架，结合概率预测器与共形校准，生成有效且高效的预测区间，支持模块化组合与自适应校准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有共形预测方法对预测器-非一致性分数的组合缺乏统一框架，难以在不同不确定性条件下自适应校准预测区间。

Method: 采用数值反演方法构建区间边界，支持任意概率预测器（MC dropout、深度集成、分位数回归）与任意非一致性分数搭配；引入改进的Winkler分数平衡有效性与效率。

Result: 在合成和真实时间序列数据上，DCP能自适应校准预测区间，优于或泛化现有方法（如CQR、CMC）。

Conclusion: DCP模块化设计为动态环境和高风险应用中的不确定性量化提供了可扩展的基础框架。

Abstract: We present Distribution-aware Conformal Prediction (DCP), a unified framework integrating probabilistic predictors like Monte Carlo dropout, deep ensembles, and quantile regression with score-agnostic conformal calibration to produce valid and efficient prediction intervals. Leveraging a numerical inversion approach to construct interval bounds, DCP accommodates arbitrary combinations of distribution generating predictors and nonconformity scores. Benchmark analysis on synthetic and real-world time series data demonstrate DCP's ability to adaptively calibrate prediction intervals under varying uncertainty regimes. Crucially, DCP's modular design facilitates plug-and-play experimentation with different predictor-score pairings, quantitatively supported by a newly introduced modified Winkler score that balances validity and efficiency by explicitly penalizing undercoverage. While DCP generalizes and extends existing approaches like Conformalized Quantile Regression and Conformalized Monte Carlo, its modular design allows further extensions, setting a foundation for advancing uncertainty quantification in dynamic environments and high-risk applications.

</details>


### [139] [Separate Aggregation of Split Network for Personalized Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26571)
*Yunseok Kang,Jaeyoung Song*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PGFedSplit，一种通过拆分架构和自适应聚合调度来增强个性化与全局泛化的联邦学习框架，在严重客户端异质性下表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有个性化联邦学习方法在全局共享与局部适应之间存在根本性权衡：强全局共享削弱局部特化，强局部适应则导致过拟合，尤其在数据有限、标签不平衡和类别缺失场景下问题突出。

Method: 采用拆分架构，根据模型组件角色进行自适应聚合调度；客户端融合本地提取表示与服务器端高斯统计生成的合成表示，增强鲁棒性。

Result: 在Fashion MNIST、CIFAR-10、CIFAR-100和Tiny ImageNet上，PGFedSplit在高度异构设置下持续优于现有方法，收敛稳定，个性化效果显著。

Conclusion: PGFedSplit有效缓解了全局共享与局部适应之间的冲突，在严重客户端异质性下同时提升了个性化性能和全局泛化能力。

Abstract: Federated learning enables collaborative model training without sharing raw data, but its performance can degrade substantially under heterogeneous client data distributions. A single global model often cannot satisfy diverse client requirements, so personalized federated learning has therefore been explored to improve client specific performance while preserving global generalization. Existing PFL methods often face a fundamental tradeoff in which stronger global sharing can undermine local specialization, whereas stronger local adaptation can lead to overfitting under limited data, label imbalance, and missing class scenarios. In this work, we propose PGFedSplit, a personalized federated learning framework that improves both personalization and global generalization under severe client heterogeneity. PGFedSplit adopts a split architecture and performs adaptive aggregation scheduling tailored to the roles of different model components, enabling stable knowledge sharing while maintaining client specific adaptation. Each client further leverages a mixture of locally extracted representations and synthetic representations generated from server side Gaussian statistics, improving robustness under label imbalance and missing class conditions. Extensive experiments on Fashion MNIST, CIFAR 10, CIFAR 100, and Tiny ImageNet demonstrate consistent improvements over state of the art PFL methods, with stable convergence and superior personalization in highly heterogeneous settings.

</details>


### [140] [Focal Reward: Balanced Reinforcement Learning under Rubric-Based Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26579)
*Yu Huang,Zihua Zhao,Zhaoxin Huan,Wanli Gu,Feng Hong,Xinmu Ge,Lin Yuan,Weichang Wu,Qiang Hu,Xiaolu Zhang,Jun Zhou,Jiangchao Yao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Focal Reward方法，通过逆奖励投影机制估计各评估维度的饱和程度，并自动重新加权损失系数，解决强化学习训练中奖励极化导致的不平衡问题。在18个模型-基准对比中全面超越静态聚合基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM开放式生成任务中，基于多维评价标准的强化学习存在奖励极化问题：各维度奖励不平衡导致模型在某些维度训练不足，即使整体奖励高，用户体验仍会恶化。

Method: 1）逆奖励投影机制估计每个评价标准的饱和程度；2）基于饱和程度自动重新分配各标准的权重系数，实现细粒度平衡训练。

Result: 在3种模型规模和6个基准上，Focal Reward在全部18个模型-基准对比中优于最强的静态聚合基线。消融实验和机制分析验证了在线、饱和感知的权重重分配是性能提升的关键。

Conclusion: Focal Reward通过动态感知各维度饱和程度并自动调整训练权重，有效解决了奖励极化问题，显著提升了LLM在开放生成任务中的多维度表现。

Abstract: The open-ended generation in LLMs usually requires multi-dimensional rubrics to adequately assess quality and guide the improvement of reinforcement learning. However, a critical dilemma inherent in this training paradigm is the imbalanced reward polarization along different rubric dimensions. Under this bottleneck, even if LLMs achieve relatively high rewards after training, they may still exhibit severe deficiencies in certain dimensions, leading to a direct deterioration in user experience. To address this problem, we propose Focal Reward, a novel objective to automatically balance the training of reinforcement learning under rubric-based rewards. Specifically, we first leverage an inverse reward projection mechanism to estimate the saturation degree of each criterion in the rubric, which forms the basis to calibrate the reward direction. Then, the final objective is designed with an automatically reweighting coefficient for each criterion to achieve the fine-grained balancing. Extensive experiments across three model scales and six benchmarks demonstrate that our Focal Reward method outperforms the strongest static aggregation baseline in all 18 model-benchmark comparisons. Rollout, mechanism, and ablation analyses further show that these gains arise from online, saturation-aware reallocation toward rubrics that still have room for improvement.

</details>


### [141] [On the Error-Correcting Effects of Stochasticity in Discrete Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26582)
*William Yuan,Sungwon Jeong,Amirali Aghazadeh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了离散扩散模型中马尔可夫转移的随机性程度对采样效率与质量的权衡影响，提出DCRS算法通过注入受控随机性来改进低函数评估次数下的速度-质量权衡，在图像数据集上实现高达10倍采样步骤缩减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散扩散模型在文本和图像生成中表现优异，但推理速度慢，且采样效率与样本质量之间存在固有权衡。现有方法未能深入理解随机性程度如何影响这一权衡，缺乏理论指导下的优化方案。

Method: 1) 通过信息论分析识别出"冗余转移"的误差纠正机制；2) 提出离散块与重启采样（DCRS）算法，在正向和反向扩散过程之间交替注入受控随机性。

Result: 在合成数据集和大规模基准上，DCRS在低函数评估次数下改善了速度-质量权衡。图像数据集上实现高达10倍采样步骤缩减且保持竞争力；语言基准上的表现更依赖于具体的破坏过程和采样程序。

Conclusion: 高度确定性的转移收敛快但误差累积严重，而更随机的转移收敛慢但能达到更高最终质量。受控随机性可通过冗余转移实现误差纠正，从而优化采样效率与质量的权衡。

Abstract: Discrete diffusion models achieve strong performance in text and image generation, but their inference remains slow and must inherently balance sampling efficiency and sample quality. In this work, we present a systematic study of how the \emph{degree of stochasticity} in Markov transitions governs the sampling tradeoff. We show that highly deterministic transitions converge rapidly but suffer from error accumulation, while more stochastic transitions converge more slowly yet can achieve higher final sample quality. Using an information-theoretic analysis, we identify the underlying mechanism as an error-correcting effect induced by \emph{redundant transitions} that symmetrically exchange mass between states, and show that these transitions can provably contract sampling errors. Motivated by this analysis, we propose \emph{Discrete Churn and Restart Sampling} (DCRS), a novel inference algorithm that injects controlled stochasticity by alternating between forward and reverse diffusion processes. Experiments on synthetic datasets and large-scale benchmarks show that DCRS improves the speed-quality tradeoff in the low number of function evaluations regime. On image datasets, DCRS achieves up to a $10\times$ reduction in sampling steps compared to standard samplers while maintaining competitive sample quality, whereas on language benchmarks, we observe more nuanced behavior depending on the corruption process and sampling procedure.

</details>


### [142] [Near-Optimal Regret in Adversarial Kernel Bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26585)
*Yu-Jie Zhang,Hao Qiu,Jonathan Scarlett,Kevin Jamieson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对对抗性核赌博机问题，提出了一种基于指数加权算法和正则化重要性加权损失估计器的方法，通过显式校正项消除正则化带来的偏差，实现了与随机核赌博机问题已知最优率相匹配的遗憾界，并在Matérn核上显著改进了现有结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对抗性核赌博机问题的遗憾上界不够理想，特别是对于Matérn核，存在次优的遗憾界；同时，先前工作依赖于排名第一的对手假设，限制了方法的适用性。本文旨在消除这一假设并提升遗憾界。

Method: 提出了一种指数加权算法，其核心是带正则化的重要性加权损失估计器，并引入显式校正项来补偿正则化导致的偏差。通过有效维度d_*(λ)刻画核复杂度，进而推导遗憾界。

Result: 遗憾界为$\widetilde{O}\big(\sqrt{T\, d_*(λ)\,\log|{X}|}\big)$，在Matérn核上达到$\widetilde{O}\big(T^{(ν+d)/(2ν+d)}\big)$，匹配随机核赌博机的最优率，且与并发工作的下界仅差$\log T$因子。

Conclusion: 本文提出的方法在对抗性环境下达到了与随机环境相同的遗憾率，且无需排名第一的对手假设，显著改进了现有结果，为对抗性核赌博机问题提供了接近最优的解决方案。

Abstract: We study the adversarial kernel bandit problem, in which the loss at each round is induced by an arbitrary bounded element of a reproducing kernel Hilbert space (RKHS). We propose an exponential-weights algorithm built on a regularized importance-weighted loss estimator, together with an explicit correction term that cancels the bias introduced by the regularization. Our main result bounds the regret by $\widetilde{O}\big(\sqrt{T\, d_*(λ)\,\log|{X}|}\big)$, where $d_*(λ)$ is a widely-adopted notion of effective dimension that captures the complexity of the kernel. Up to logarithmic factors, this matches the known rate achieved in the related stochastic kernel bandit problem. A notable application is the Matérn$(ν,d)$ kernel with smoothness parameter $ν$ on $\mathbb{R}^d$, for which our bound specializes to $\widetilde{O}\big(T^{(ν+d)/(2ν+d)}\big)$, improving over the best-known prior rate of Chatterji et al. [2019] while simultaneously removing the rank-one adversary assumption required by their analysis. Moreover, this rate is the same as the known optimal rate for stochastic kernel bandits, and also matches a lower bound from concurrent work up to a $\log T$ factor.

</details>


### [143] [Few-shot Cross-country Generalization of Tabular Machine Learning and Foundation Models for Childhood Anemia Prediction under Distribution Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26589)
*Yusuf Brima,Marcellin Atemkeng,Lansana Hassim Kallon,David Niyukuri,Antoine Vacavant,Samuel Saidu,Ding-Geng Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文评估了基于Transformer的表格基础模型TabPFN在儿童贫血预测中的表现，发现在低数据场景下优于传统方法，但模型选择的影响小于人群异质性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 儿童贫血影响全球约40%的6-59个月儿童，其成因复杂多样，导致模型泛化能力受限，亟需评估基础模型在跨国家和数据稀缺场景下的适用性。

Method: 使用16个国家的DHS数据（n=68,856），比较Logistic回归、XGBoost、LightGBM和TabPFN v2.6，通过LOCO、反向LOCO和少样本设置评估泛化能力，并利用SHAP分析特征重要性。

Result: TabPFN在低数据场景（<200样本）下表现最优，AUC-ROC为0.59-0.76，Brier分数最低（0.042），ECE为0.203；LOCO性能稳定（0.58-0.69），关键预测因子为儿童年龄、海拔和身高别体重Z评分。

Conclusion: 儿童贫血预测性能主要受人群差异驱动，而非模型选择；TabPFN在低资源场景下具有优势，基础模型是数据稀缺的全球健康预测的有前景工具。

Abstract: Childhood anemia affects around 40% of children aged 6-59 months globally and arises from heterogeneous factors, limiting model generalizability. We evaluate a transformer-based tabular foundation model against classical supervised methods under cross-country and data-scarce settings.
  We used DHS data from 16 countries across Africa, Asia, Latin America, the Caucasus, and the Middle East (n=68,856). We compared Logistic Regression, XGBoost, LightGBM, and TabPFN v2.6. Performance was assessed using AUC-ROC, Brier score, and ECE. Generalization was evaluated using leave-one-country-out (LOCO), reverse-LOCO, and few-shot settings. Subgroup analyses included sex, age, residence, maternal education, and wealth. Feature importance was estimated using SHAP.
  TabPFN outperformed classical models in low-data regimes (<200 samples), showing higher discrimination and better calibration. Across countries, it achieved the lowest Brier score (0.042) and ECE (0.203). Under full-data settings, AUC-ROC ranged from 0.59-0.76 with small between-model differences ($\leq 0.05$). LOCO performance was stable (0.58-0.69), driven by country context. Reverse-LOCO showed asymmetric transferability. Subgroup performance was consistent with no systematic demographic bias. SHAP identified child age, altitude, and height-for-age z-score as dominant predictors, followed by wealth and maternal education.
  Performance in childhood anemia prediction is driven more by population variation than model choice. TabPFN provides advantages in low-resource settings through improved discrimination and calibration, highlighting foundation models as promising tools for data-scarce global health prediction.

</details>


### [144] [Spend Your Rollouts Where It Counts: Rollout Allocation for Group-Based RL Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26606)
*Woojeong Kim,Ziyi Yang,Jing Nathan Yan,Jialu Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Pilot-Commit框架，通过预算感知的 rollout 分配策略，在群组强化学习中节省计算成本，实现更高效的策略优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线强化学习中，rollout生成占据了训练的主要计算开销。现有群组策略优化方法为所有prompt分配相同预算，即使某些prompt的奖励分布已经收敛，造成大量计算浪费。

Method: 提出Pilot-Commit框架，将prompt评估与利用解耦：先用部分预算（pilot阶段）估计每个prompt的信息量，然后将剩余rollout分配给高信号prompt，跳过低信号prompt。

Result: 在多个数学推理基准和1.5B到14B参数规模下，Pilot-Commit在保持基线精度的同时显著降低采样成本，累计rollout速度比GRPO快1.9倍，比DAPO快4.0倍。

Conclusion: 群组更新在奖励方差高的区域最有效，因此需要在线动态估计prompt的信息量，Pilot-Commit通过预算感知分配实现了更高效的群组RL后训练。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) is the dominant paradigm for post-training large language models. However, in the online, on-policy setting, rollout generation dominates the computational cost of training. Group-based policy optimization methods compute advantages from multiple rollouts per prompt, yet they indiscriminately allocate budget to prompts with collapsed reward distributions, wasting expensive rollouts on negligible learning signals. We demonstrate that group-based updates are most effective in regimes of high reward variance. Since the policy evolves throughout training, prompt informativeness must be estimated online rather than precomputed, but exhaustively evaluating every prompt is computationally prohibitive. We introduce Pilot-Commit, a budget-aware rollout allocation framework for group-based RL post-training. Pilot-Commit decouples prompt evaluation from exploitation: a pilot stage estimates per-prompt informativeness using a fraction of the budget, and the remaining rollouts are allocated to high-leverage prompts while low-signal prompts are skipped. Across multiple math reasoning benchmarks and model scales from 1.5B to 14B parameters, Pilot-Commit matches baseline accuracy with significantly lower sampling costs, reaching target accuracy up to $1.9\times$ faster than GRPO and $4.0\times$ faster than DAPO in cumulative rollouts.

</details>


### [145] [PIDM-DP: Physics-Informed Diffusion with Dormand-Prince Integration for Chaotic System Identification and State Reconstruction across Multiple Dynamical Regimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26619)
*Shailendra Dabral*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出PIDM-DP模型，将5阶Dormand-Prince ODE求解器嵌入扩散模型逆向采样中，通过物理残差反向传播约束轨迹满足混沌系统方程，在稀疏噪声观测下实现比未约束扩散模型最高15.4倍的RMSE提升，并在刚性问题中显著优于EnKF。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从稀疏、带噪声的观测中重建混沌动力系统的连续状态轨迹是非线性科学中的一个基本开放问题，现有方法在刚性和高维系统中面临精度和稳定性挑战。

Method: 将可微分的5阶Dormand-Prince ODE积分器嵌入DDPM逆向采样循环，在每个去噪步骤通过自动微分反向传播物理残差；采用线性调度引导机制，从高噪声时物理权重为零逐渐增加到干净数据附近的全值，避免梯度爆炸。

Result: 在5个基准系统（3D Lorenz、3D Rössler、5D超混沌、20D Lorenz-96、刚性3D Rabinovich-Fabrikant）上，以10%观测密度和加性高斯噪声（σ=0.05）评估，RMSE改进最高达15.4倍；在Rabinovich-Fabrikant分布外基准上，PIDM-DP的RMSE为0.1097±0.0269，对比未约束扩散（0.9443±0.5288，差8.6倍）和EnKF（0.3561±0.3040，差3.2倍），p<0.001。

Conclusion: PIDM-DP在混沌系统状态重建中显著优于未约束扩散和EnKF，尤其适用于刚性问题，并能保持混沌不变测度。

Abstract: Reconstructing continuous state trajectories of chaotic dynamical systems from sparse, noisy observations remains a fundamental open problem in nonlinear science. We introduce the Physics-Informed Diffusion Model with Dormand-Prince Integration (PIDM-DP), which embeds a fully differentiable 5th-order Dormand-Prince (DP-RK45) ODE integrator directly into the reverse sampling loop of a Denoising Diffusion Probabilistic Model (DDPM). At each denoising step, physics residuals are back-propagated via automatic differentiation, constraining every generated trajectory to satisfy the system's governing equations to 5th-order accuracy. A linear-scheduled guidance mechanism that ramps the physics weight from zero at high noise levels to its full value near the clean-data limit prevents the gradient explosions that cause naive physics-informed approaches to fail on stiff systems with Jacobian eigenvalues of order $O(10^3)$. Evaluated across five benchmark systems of increasing complexity 3D Lorenz, 3D Rössler, 5D Hyperchaotic, 20D Lorenz-96, and the stiff 3D Rabinovich-Fabrikant at 10% observation density with additive Gaussian noise ($σ=0.05$), PIDM-DP achieves reconstruction RMSE improvements of up to $15.4\times$ over an unconstrained diffusion baseline and decisively outperforms the Ensemble Kalman Filter on stiff systems where ensemble covariance collapses. On the Rabinovich-Fabrikant out-of-distribution benchmark, PIDM-DP attains RMSE $0.1097 \pm 0.0269$ versus $0.9443 \pm 0.5288$ (unconstrained diffusion, $8.6\times$ worse) and $0.3561 \pm 0.3040$ (EnKF, $3.2\times$ worse), with $p<0.001$ in paired Wilcoxon tests ($N = 30$). Topological validation via the Rosenstein Lyapunov estimator confirms that PIDM-DP preserves the chaotic invariant measure.

</details>


### [146] [RT-Lynx: Putting the GEMM Sparsity In a Right Way for Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26632)
*Xing Cong,Hanlin Tang,Kan Liu,Lan Tao,Lin Qu,Chenhao Xie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RT-Lynx方法，将N:M半结构化稀疏从权重转移到激活值上，结合误差补偿技术，实现DiT模型的高效推理加速，在保持生成质量的同时平均加速1.55倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量化、蒸馏等方法已用于降低DiT推理成本，但半结构化稀疏（可减半FLOPs）在权重剪枝中会破坏模型容量与生成质量，未被充分探索。本文发现激活值天然稀疏且对稀疏化更鲁棒，因此推动从权重稀疏化到激活稀疏化的范式转变。

Method: 提出RT-Lynx，对激活值应用N:M半结构化稀疏，并结合误差补偿技术降低精度损失；同时设计了面向该场景的高度优化的CUDA内核。

Result: 在线性层上平均实现1.55倍加速，在多个扩散模型上保持了与原始模型相当的生成质量。

Conclusion: 激活值稀疏化相比权重稀疏化在DiT中更有效，RT-Lynx能在不牺牲生成质量的前提下显著加速推理，为扩散模型的高效部署提供了新思路。

Abstract: Diffusion Transformers (DiT) achieve strong performance in image generation but incur substantial inference costs. While prior work has reduced this cost via quantization and distillation, semi-structured sparsity, which can nearly halve FLOPs, remains underexplored. A key reason is that most existing approaches focus on weight sparsification, and pruning 50% of the weights can remove critical model capacity and degrade generation quality. Our study, however, shows that DiT activations are intrinsically sparse and significantly more robust to N:M semi-structured sparsification than weights. Motivated by this observation, we advocate a paradigm shift from weight sparsification to activation sparsification. We propose RT-Lynx, which applies N:M sparsification to activations and incorporates error-compensation techniques to mitigate accuracy loss. We further implement highly optimized CUDA kernels tailored to this setting, achieving up to a 1.55x speedup on average in linear layers. Extensive experiments across multiple diffusion models demonstrate that our method preserves the generation quality of the original models while substantially accelerating inference.

</details>


### [147] [More Expressive Feedforward Layers: Part I. Token-Adaptive Mixing of Activations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26647)
*Mingze Wang,Jinbo Wang,Yikuan Xia,Kai Shen,Shu Zhong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出混合激活函数（MoA）方法，通过轻量级输入依赖门控混合多个激活函数，使FFN层具有token自适应性，在LLM中显著提升表达能力与扩展性能，且参数和计算开销极小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Transformer FFN层使用单一固定激活函数（如ReLU、GELU、SwiGLU），对所有token施加相同非线性变换，限制了模型表达能力。作者希望设计一种token自适应的激活机制以提升FFN表达能力。

Method: 提出Mixture of Activations（MoA）：在共享线性投影的FFN中，通过轻量级输入依赖门控混合字典中多个激活函数。同时提出输入无关的Learnable Activations（LA）作为对比基线。理论上证明了MoA严格包含LA，LA严格包含固定激活FFN的表达能力层次。

Result: 在0.12B到2B参数的密集和MoE语言模型上，MoA在不同token预算、优化器和学习率调度下均一致取得更低的终端损失，并展现出更优的扩展行为，参数和计算开销极小。

Conclusion: token自适应激活混合是一种简单有效的机制，能够在不引入显著开销的情况下提升LLM FFN层的表达能力。

Abstract: Feedforward network (FFN) layers account for a large fraction of parameters and nonlinear expressivity in Transformer-based large language models (LLMs). Despite the evolution from ReLU and GELU to gated variants such as SwiGLU, most FFN designs still use a single fixed activation function, applying the same nonlinear transformation to all tokens. In this work, we propose Mixture of Activations (MoA), a token-adaptive FFN design that mixes a dictionary of activation functions using lightweight input-dependent gates while sharing the same linear projections. As an input-independent counterpart, we also introduce learnable activations (LA), which form linear combinations of activation functions for both ReLU-type and SwiGLU-type FFNs. Theoretically, we establish strict finite-width expressive separations among fixed-activation FFNs, LA, and MoA: LA strictly contains fixed-activation FFNs, while MoA strictly contains LA, with the additional expressivity arising from input-dependent nonlinear hybridization. Empirically, we evaluate MoA through extensive pre-training experiments on dense and MoE language models ranging from 0.12B to 2B parameters under different token budgets, optimizers, and learning rate schedules. MoA consistently achieves lower terminal loss and exhibits more favorable scaling behavior than well-tuned baselines, with minimal parameter and computational overhead. These results suggest that token-adaptive activation mixing is a simple and effective mechanism for improving FFN expressivity in LLMs.

</details>


### [148] [Bilevel Optimization over Saddle Points of Zero-Sum Markov Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26654)
*Zihao Zheng,Irwin King,Songtao Lu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PANDA算法，一种基于惩罚项和Nikaido-Isoda函数的一阶策略梯度方法，用于解决下层为min-max零和马尔可夫博弈的双层强化学习问题，无需凸性假设即可收敛到驻点，在理论上匹配最优收敛率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有双层强化学习方法假定下层问题为单策略马尔可夫决策过程，无法处理激励设计等应用中存在的多策略竞争结构。本文旨在解决下层为min-max零和马尔可夫博弈的双层优化问题。

Method: 提出PANDA（penalty-augmented Nikaido-Isoda descent-ascent）算法，这是一种基于惩罚项的Nikaido-Isoda函数的一阶策略梯度方法，利用min-max博弈结构避免计算上层超梯度且无需二阶信息。

Result: PANDA无需UL或LL凸性假设即可收敛到驻点，达到ε-驻点需要Õ(ε⁻¹)次迭代，样本复杂度为Õ(ε⁻³)，匹配现有单策略下层MDP双层RL的最优已知速率。

Conclusion: PANDA有效解决了下层为竞争性多策略博弈的双层RL问题，理论上具有最优收敛保证，实验上优于相关基线方法。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) often has a hierarchical structure, where an upper-level (UL) learner selects model parameters and a lower-level (LL) decision-making process responds, naturally leading to a bilevel optimization problem. Most existing bilevel RL methods assume a single-policy LL Markov decision process (MDP), and therefore fail to capture competitive structures arising in applications such as incentive design, where multiple policies interact. We study bilevel optimization problems in which the LL problem is a regularized min-max zero-sum Markov game and the UL objective is optimized through the saddle-point equilibrium induced by the LL game. In this work, we propose penalty-augmented Nikaido-Isoda descent-ascent (PANDA), a penalty-based first-order policy-gradient method based on the Nikaido-Isoda function. By exploiting the min-max game structure, PANDA avoids computing UL hypergradients and does not require second-order information. We prove that PANDA converges to stationary points without convexity assumptions on either the UL or LL objectives. Moreover, PANDA reaches an $ε$-stationary point in $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(ε^{-1})$ iterations with sample complexity $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(ε^{-3})$, matching the best-known rates for bilevel RL with single-policy LL MDPs. Experiments demonstrate the superior performance of PANDA over closely related baselines.

</details>


### [149] [WINDQuant: Weight-Informed Neural Decision-Making for Global Mixed-Precision LLM Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26660)
*Phong Nam Huu Nguyen,Khoi M. Le,Cong-Duy T Nguyen,Anh Tuan Luu,Thong Thanh Nguyen,Tho Quan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出WINDQuant方法，利用强化学习（PPO）为LLM的细粒度列块分配混合精度比特位宽，在超低位宽下实现高效量化，避免重新训练，并显著降低精度损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有后训练量化在超低位宽下精度严重下降，而量化感知训练需要高昂的重训成本；现有混合精度策略多基于粗粒度或启发式敏感性分析，忽略权重矩阵内部的细粒度差异。

Method: 提出WINDQuant，以强化学习（PPO）为核心控制器，在全局存储预算下为每个细粒度列块学习比特位宽和量化处理方式；结合激活感知校准、轻量级逐单元量化器拟合以及显式有效比特计算。

Result: 在LLaMA模型上的实验表明，WINDQuant在超低位宽设置下取得了有竞争力的性能，同时相比基于重训练的方法降低了优化开销。

Conclusion: 强化学习可作为自适应混合精度量化的一种实用控制器，兼顾性能与效率。

Abstract: Quantization is an effective approach to reduce the memory footprint and inference cost of large language models (LLMs), yet maintaining performance in the ultra-low-bit regime remains challenging. Existing post-training methods often suffer from severe accuracy degradation, while quantization-aware training requires costly retraining and additional resources. Moreover, most mixed-precision strategies rely on coarse-grained or heuristic sensitivity analysis that overlooks fine-grained variations within weight matrices. We propose WINDQuant, a reinforcement-learning-based allocation controller for ultra-low-bit LLM quantization. Rather than introducing another low-level quantization operator, WINDQuant learns how to assign bit-widths and quantization treatments to fine-grained column chunks under a global storage budget. By operating at the column-chunk level, WINDQuant enables flexible and fine-grained precision assignment within layers under a global target bit-width. The implementation combines PPO with activation-aware calibration, lightweight per-unit quantizer fitting, and explicit effective-bit accounting of the learned mixed-precision plan. Experiments on LLaMA models demonstrate that WINDQuant achieves competitive performance in ultra-low-bit settings while reducing optimization overhead relative to retraining-based approaches, highlighting reinforcement learning as a practical controller for adaptive mixed-precision quantization.

</details>


### [150] [Beyond Trajectory-Level Attribution: Graph-Based Credit Assignment for Agentic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26684)
*Xin Cheng,Shuo He,Lang Feng,HaiYang Xu,Ming Yan,Lei Feng,Bo An*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出GraphGPO方法，通过将轨迹聚合为状态转移图并基于图距离进行细粒度信用分配，提升LLM在智能体任务中的训练效率与性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于组的强化学习方法在信用分配上依赖粗粒度的轨迹级归因，难以捕捉单个步骤的贡献（如失败轨迹中的有用步骤），需要更细粒度的步骤级信用分配方法。

Method: 首先将所有 rollout 轨迹聚合成统一的状态转移图，然后利用图中全局信息估计每个状态到任务目标的距离，最后基于每一步转移减少目标距离的程度来分配图优势值（graph-based advantage），实现步骤级信用分配。

Result: 在一系列具有挑战性的基准测试中取得了最先进的性能，并显著提升了训练效率。

Conclusion: GraphGPO通过图结构编码全局信息，实现了更精准的步骤级信用分配，有效提升了强化学习训练效果。

Abstract: Group-based reinforcement learning (RL) methods have achieved remarkable success in improving the performance of large language models (LLMs) and have been rapidly extended to agentic tasks. However, their credit assignment relies heavily on coarse-grained trajectory-level attribution according to final outcomes, making it difficult to capture the contribution of individual steps, such as valuable steps obscured within failed trajectories. To uncover latent information and enable more faithful step-level credit assignment, we propose Graph-based Group Policy Optimization (GraphGPO), which first aggregates all rollout trajectories into a unified state-transition graph and then estimates the distance from each state to the task goal using the global information encoded in the graph. Finally, GraphGPO assigns credit to each edge by estimating a graph-based advantage, based on how much the transition reduces the distance to the task goal. In this way, GraphGPO significantly improves training efficiency and achieves state-of-the-art performance across a range of challenging benchmarks.

</details>


### [151] [SL-BiLEM: Structured Learnable Behavior-in-the-Loop Epidemic Modeling for Forecasting and Policy Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26704)
*Haochun Wang,Sendong Zhao,Jingbo Wang,Yanrui Du,Bing Qin,Ting Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SL-BiLEM框架，通过物理约束（单调性、平滑性、有界跳跃）正则化学习人类行为反馈，解决流行病预测中政策干预导致的分布偏移问题，在真实数据预测与反事实分析上均优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流行病预测面临根本性挑战：人类行为会动态响应疾病传播，形成反馈循环，在政策干预点引发分布偏移，导致数据驱动模型不可靠。

Method: 将有效传播率分解为基准传染率、政策效应、媒体效应和依从性函数的乘积，并对依从性函数施加单调性、平滑性和有界跳跃约束，利用物理约束作为正则化实现鲁棒外推。

Result: 相比神经机制模型提升76%；政策分布偏移下OOD退化仅53%（神经基线为1142%）；27个合成反事实实验中100% bootstrap CI覆盖；处理效应准确度超过0.85。

Conclusion: SL-BiLEM为公共卫生决策者提供了一个可解释的工具，能够同时实现准确预测和原则性的干预规划。

Abstract: Epidemic forecasting faces a fundamental challenge: human behavior dynamically responds to disease spread, creating feedback loops that induce distribution shifts at policy intervention points. This renders data-driven models unreliable under distribution shift. We propose \textbf{SL-BiLEM} (Structured Learnable Behavior-in-the-Loop Epidemic Model), leveraging physical constraints as regularization for robust extrapolation. The framework decomposes effective transmission as $β_{\text{eff}}(t,g) = β_0(g) \times m_{\text{policy}}(t) \times m_{\text{media}}(t) \times m_{\text{comp}}(t,g)$, where monotonicity, smoothness, and bounded-jump constraints on the learned compliance function maintain predictive validity under novel policy regimes. Beyond forecasting, SL-BiLEM enables counterfactual analysis for intervention decision support. We validate forecasting on three real-world datasets (cruise ship, school influenza, and school-district COVID-19 surveillance) and evaluate counterfactual recovery on synthetic benchmarks with known ground truth. SL-BiLEM demonstrates: (1) 76\% improvement over neural-mechanistic baselines, with only 53\% OOD degradation versus 1142\% for neural baselines under policy-induced shift; (2) 100\% bootstrap CI coverage across 27 synthetic counterfactual experiments; and (3) Treatment Effect Accuracy exceeding 0.85. These results establish SL-BiLEM as an interpretable tool for public health decision-makers seeking accurate prediction and principled intervention planning.

</details>


### [152] [Image Feature Fusion-based Federated Client Unlearning (FCU)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26715)
*Hangyi Shen,Yizhi Pan,Tiansuo Li,Weiqi Jiang,Guanqun Sun*

Main category: cs.LG
